# P9.3 red and green records

Plan: the approved P9.3 design (v6) and test plan (v6), baseline lane,
steps B1 to B3. Each new test in this file ran twice: once with its named
break applied (red) and once on the committed code (green). The break is
one exact text replacement, shown as a diff, applied to a copy of the
tree and reverted. The red lines are the failing assertion and the
nextest summary. The green line is the nextest summary after the revert.

Reproduce one record:

```sh
mise exec -- cargo nextest run --locked --no-fail-fast \
  -p jackin-console --test <file> -E 'test(=<name>)'
```

Apply the break first, run, revert it, run again. Breaks touch library
source only, never an approved baseline. After the revert, touch the file
so the build sees it as changed.

Gate after each step: `cargo nextest run --locked --all-targets`,
`cargo clippy --locked --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `cargo fmt --check`,
and an empty `git diff --stat origin/feature/openshell-native --
tests/functional/baselines apps/jackin/tests/functional/baselines`.
## B1: fixtures in the library, World::new (BX1, BX12)

Tests: `apps/jackin/tests/world.rs`.

#### `every_scenario_builds_a_world_that_names_it`: world_for FirstUse returns the populated world

```diff

-            let mut w = base_world(scenario);
-            w.discovery_pending = true;
```

```text
assertion failed: world_for(Scenario::FirstUse).workspaces.is_empty()
Summary 1 test run: 0 passed, 1 failed, 4 skipped
```

Green (exit 0):

```text
Summary 1 test run: 1 passed, 4 skipped
```

## B4-0: the fixed state, the refusal slot, reason widths, the fixed-controls page (BX10 library part, BX17)

Tests: `apps/jackin/tests/fixed.rs`, `reason.rs`, `account_data.rs` and, for the page, `tests/functional/showcase.rs` (`showcase_fixed_controls_*`, `showcase_page_fixedcontrols`). The in-process tests render library widgets into a test buffer and drive them with keys and clicks. The page tests run the `showcase` binary in a pseudo-terminal. Each break ran in a copy of the tree. The records of `a_blocked_edit_leaves_the_other_actions_open` add to its B3 records; the test now sets a `Reason` and checks the menu border.

Reproduce a page record with `cargo nextest run --locked --test functional --no-tests fail -E 'test(=showcase::<name>)'`. The page baseline `showcase_page_fixedcontrols.tui.json` is new and was accepted with the Tuiscotti CLI; the break that renames the checkbox goes red against it.

One break was dropped: making the refusal use the error tone does not turn the page test red, because the error status clears on the next key and `advance_clock` is a paste. The in-process test `a_refusal_posts_the_reason_alone_in_a_neutral_tone` owns that claim.

## B4-0 review round: the flaky leak test and the fixes behind it

`showcase_fixed_controls_refuse_with_a_reason_and_never_leak_it` failed on the
unmodified commit `a1435ba`: 3 failures in 21 isolated runs, and 7 of 12 when run
with the other two fixed-control tests. The failing step was always `key space`
on the `Typed key` radio option, `timed out: status reads Not available · managed
by policy`.

Cause: the page returned `Outcome::Consumed` after a refusal, the showcase host did
not turn a posted status into `Changed`, and the runtime redraws only on `Changed`.
The new status painted only if a pointer flash or a status expiry repainted first.
The jackin console was not affected (its `apply_requests` returns `Changed`).

Fix, in the shared layer so no page has to remember it: `shell::handle` compares the
status line (text and tone) before and after each input and ORs in `Changed`; the
showcase host returns `Changed` when a page posts any request. The slot is also
cleared at the start of `handle`, not only at the end.

The tests that pin this round (keyboard-only redraw, the ListItem paths, the exact
neutral tone, the sticky status, the Dialog fixed action) are recorded in the next
section.

## B4-0 fix round: the review fixes, the library tests and the page tests

Plan: the B4-0 fix test plan, rows T1 to T15 and the unpinned vocabulary strings, as amended by its review (A1 to A16). Each record below is one break and one test: the break is applied to the tree by a script, the named test runs red, the file is restored and the same test runs green. A break is one edit, or a named group of edits when the code has two guards for the same behaviour (the records say which). Every red line is the assertion that failed, with its expected and actual values. Breaks touch library or console source only, never an approved baseline.

The tests of the library's own types moved out of `apps/jackin/tests` into the library's root `tests/`: `tests/fixed.rs` and `tests/reason.rs` (the 15 tests that were `apps/jackin/tests/fixed.rs` and `reason.rs`, minus `the_shell_clears_an_untaken_refusal_at_the_end_of_each_input`, which is rewritten on a `shell::App` double in `tests/shell.rs`), with the helpers in `tests/common/mod.rs`. The records of the section "B4-0" above were taken when those files lived under `apps/jackin/tests/`; the test names are unchanged and the line numbers are those of that location. A move has no break; the 15 moved tests run in `tests/fixed.rs` and `tests/reason.rs` and pass.

Run a library record with `cargo nextest run --locked -p junie-tui --test <file> -E 'test(=<name>)'` and a console record with `-p jackin-console --test <file>`.

Rows written and kept: every test in these records turns red under at least one break.

Rows dropped, because no break turns them red or the premise is gone:

- T3, 70,000 reasons in a loop and a `Reason` sent to another thread (A3): after the slot became a plain `Option<Reason>` neither exercises code that can break. Kept: a later refusal replaces an earlier one, and the slot belongs to its thread. The old break (a `Cell<Option<u16>>` index table) does not compile and is not a red.
- T4, a reason raised and taken inside `on_input` is unaffected (A4): no edit to `shell::handle` changes it, because the host takes the slot before `handle` can clear it.
- T8, the key table over both arrow modes (A7): the existing select test holds the break, with `Left` added to the ignored keys and a `Step` select added.
- T9, `short` against `reason_room` (A8): it measures a string the test built; the break "render `text` in the border" is red in `a_blocked_edit_leaves_the_other_actions_open` (record in the section "B4-0").
- T13, the full reason at 80x24 (A10): the named break is already red in the existing test and no code caps the status.
- T14, the separate keyboard-only PTY test (A6): merged into the walk test. The plan premise that `advance_clock` expires a status in the showcase is wrong: the showcase has no `advance_clock`, so the clock step is only a paste. The walk runs with sticky reasons and clears the status with one key (`f9`); a 5 s expiry in real time is `showcase_fixed_controls_reason_fades_after_five_seconds`.
- `lifecycle.restored`, `status.purged`, the nine-status table and `destination_heading` (A14): shadowed, unreachable or already pinned. They are recorded in `docs/audits/2026-11-03-fixed-controls-showcase-page.md`.
- `unpushed` in the banned-word list (A14): subsumed by the existing `pushed`. `discard` and `{state}` were added.
- The launch picker for the current directory (section 5 of the plan): no key sequence in the `Configured` rule reaches a current-directory scope that shows `no default role`; the new 80-column assertion on the existing picker test carries the N1 text.

Survivors worth knowing: `p-select-press` and `p-select-click` (each alone) leave all page tests green, as the review predicted; `d-guard` alone leaves `a_dialog_refuses_a_fixed_action_by_every_route` green because the other routes refuse before `finish` is reached.


### Dialog: a fixed action refuses on every route

Tests: `tests/fixed.rs`. The finish guard and the per-route breaks together: a route that still goes through `Button::activate` is also caught by the guard in `Dialog::finish`, so a route break removes the guard as well and restores the route as it was before the fix.

### Showcase page: keyboard and pointer refusals in a real terminal

Tests: `tests/functional/showcase.rs`. Reproduce a record with `cargo nextest run --locked -p junie-tui --test functional --no-tests fail -E 'test(=showcase::<name>)'`. The walk test runs with `--motion paused` and `fixedcontrols.status=sticky`, so no timer can repaint for the shell and the status clears with one key. The two single-route Select breaks (`p-select-press`, `p-select-click`) leave every test green, because a pointer click delivers a press and a click and both refuse; the in-process assertions of `a_fixed_select_refuses_open_arrows_typing_and_clicks` own them.

## B4-0 fix round 2: the last red and green findings

Source: the independent red and green check of `7ed1666` (verdict CHANGES_REQUIRED, findings F1 to F5 and notes N1, N2). Each record below is one break and one test: the break is applied by a script, the named test runs red, the file is restored, and the same test runs green. Breaks touch library or console source only, never an approved baseline.

- F1: `tests/shell.rs` gains `the_same_tone_with_other_text_is_a_change`. Two neutral reasons in a row differ in their text only; the redraw rule must report `Changed`.
- F2: a code defect. The GitHub dialog posted "Copied the GitHub URL" when no repository matched and nothing was copied. `capsule.rs` now posts the new `Vocabulary::github_no_url` in the error tone when there is no URL, in the demo words and the openshell words alike. A demo flow with a matching repository is untouched, so no approved baseline moved. `the_github_dialog_copies_nothing_without_a_matching_repository` no longer pins the false status; it keeps one repository that does not match the instance, so a match-anything break turns it red. Its records, and the two Copy URL records it shares with the section "Console: GitHub dialog and mount rows", are rewritten for the new code in that section.
- F3: the capture count of `the_unexported_word_replaces_unpushed` is eight, not ten (corrected in its section). The 10-session assertion of `the_purge_dialog_follows_the_words` now has its record below (a session-count cap).
- F4: `a_prompt_refuses_a_fixed_primary_when_the_field_commits` and `a_typed_acknowledgement_refuses_a_fixed_gated_action` assert the outcome of the refused Enter: `Consumed` when the action is fixed and `Changed` when it is live. A guard that returns `Ignored` lets the key bubble to the host, and the outcome is observable through `Dialog::on_key`.
- F5: `an_unchecked_fixed_row_does_not_block_deselecting_all` presses `a` twice with an unchecked fixed row. The review's probe was green on `7ed1666`: `RowSelection::tri_state` already filters out the rows that cannot be selected (`src/core/selection.rs`), and `toggle_all` passes the same predicate. No code changed. The test only pins that filter, and the break removes it.
- N1: accepted as it is. `the_shell_clears_an_untaken_refusal_at_the_end_of_each_input` and `the_shell_clears_a_stale_refusal_before_each_input` in `tests/shell.rs` own the slot clear. The PTY test `showcase_fixed_controls_refuse_with_a_reason_and_never_leak_it` cannot produce a stale reason, because every raise in its sequence is taken in the same input, so a `handle` that never clears the slot leaves it green. This record does not claim PTY coverage of the slot clear.
- Line numbers: the records of the section "Console: lifecycle, purge, stop, project count, trust and env words" and the sections after it quote `apps/jackin/tests/vocabulary.rs` lines from before this round. Every line after `the_github_dialog_copies_nothing_without_a_matching_repository` moved down by 16.

Run a library record with `cargo nextest run --locked -p junie-tui --test <file> -E 'test(=<name>)'` and a console record with `cargo nextest run --locked -p jackin-console --features fixtures --test vocabulary -E 'test(=<name>)'`.

```diff
-            .map(|(text, tone)| (text.to_owned(), tone))
+            .map(|(_, tone)| (String::new(), tone))
```

## B4a: the Fixed data, the openshell-fixed scenario and the editor Fixed paths (BX10)

Tests: `apps/jackin/tests/functional/jackin.rs`, seven PTY key tests, one per
refusal, and four baselines. Run them with:

```sh
cargo nextest run --locked -p jackin-console --test functional -E 'test(jackin_fixed)'
```

| Test | Refusal | Baseline | Breaks |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `jackin_fixed_keep_awake_refuses_by_click_and_key` | `keep_awake` | `jackin_fixed_editor_general` | S1, S2, KA1, KA2, GP3, T1, T2 |
| `jackin_fixed_git_pull_refuses_by_key_and_click` | `git_pull` | `jackin_fixed_editor_general_refused` | S1, S2, GP1, GP2, GP3 |
| `jackin_fixed_dirty_exit_refuses_the_list_by_key_and_click` | dirty exit | none | S1, S2, DE1, DE2 |
| `jackin_fixed_working_directory_refuses_the_picker_by_key_and_click` | working directory | none | S1, S2, KA2, WD1, WD2, WD3 |
| `jackin_fixed_project_row_keeps_its_paint_and_refuses_every_edit` | Project row, Add row | `jackin_fixed_editor_project` | S1, PR1 to PR7 |
| `jackin_fixed_workspace_account_toggle_refuses_and_prefer_stays_live` | workspace account toggle | `jackin_fixed_editor_accounts` | S1, AC1 to AC4 |
| `jackin_fixed_last_allowed_role_cannot_be_unchecked` | last allowed role | none | LR1, LR2 |

Each record below is one break in production code, the failing assertion, and
the green run after the revert. The last-role refusal posts its text with
`cx.status` and not through the refusal slot, so S1 leaves that test green
(1 passed in the S1 summary). The data breaks T1 and T2 change a reason text
in `Fixed::openshell()`: the tests read the text from the same function, so
only the frozen baseline turns red. A refusal posted as a sticky status
instead of a 5 s status is not caught here: the harness clock request is a
paste, and a paste clears a sticky status, so `status_expired` cannot tell
the two apart. The status tests of the library (`tests/status.rs`) pin it.
Breaks touch library source and the fixture data only, never an approved
baseline.

Five assertion classes are pinned by one test each:

- the F10 gate: `the Project row stays legible` (contrast of the destination,
  mode and source cells against their background is at least 4.5:1, PR2) and
  `the Add row is drawn fixed` (PR6);
- the reason beside the control (KA1, GP1, DE2, WD2, PR4);
- the refusal by key and by click (KA1, GP1, DE1, WD1, PR1, PR5, AC1, LR1);
- the footer hints that omit a key that only refuses (KA2, WD3, PR3, AC2);
- the live neighbour: `p` still binds (AC3) and a role can be unchecked while
  another stays allowed (LR2).

## B4b: the Settings and env Fixed paths (BX10, BX13)

Tests: `apps/jackin/tests/functional/jackin.rs`, eight PTY key tests and six
baselines, and `apps/jackin/tests/fixed_settings.rs`, ten library data tests (eight at B4b, two more in the fix round).
Run them with:

```sh
cargo nextest run --locked -p jackin-console --test functional -E 'test(jackin_fixed_settings) | test(jackin_fixed_env) | test(jackin_fixed_workspace_env)'
cargo nextest run --locked -p jackin-console --test fixed_settings
```

| Test | Refusal or rule | Baseline | Breaks |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `jackin_fixed_settings_commits_refuse_by_key_and_click` | Co-author and DCO boxes | `jackin_fixed_settings_commits` | S1, S2, CO1 to CO5, TX1 |
| `jackin_fixed_settings_global_mounts_refuse_every_add` | global Mounts Add row | `jackin_fixed_settings_mounts` | S1, S2, MO1 to MO5, TX6 |
| `jackin_fixed_settings_trust_refuses_the_toggle_and_shows_the_source` | Trust toggle, `o` | `jackin_fixed_settings_trust` | S1, S2, TR1 to TR3, TR5 to TR7, TX2 |
| `jackin_fixed_settings_agents_refuse_every_row` | every Agents row | `jackin_fixed_settings_agents` | S1, S2, AG1 to AG7, TX3 |
| `jackin_fixed_settings_role_overrides_refuse_and_the_scope_lists_global` | global Role overrides, scope select | `jackin_fixed_settings_env` | S1, RO1 to RO7, ES5, TX4 |
| `jackin_fixed_env_sources_refuse_the_radio_options_and_the_p_key` | env sources 2 and 3, `p` | `jackin_fixed_env_form` | S1, L1, L2, ES1 to ES4, TX5 |
| `jackin_fixed_env_form_refuses_a_credential_reference_and_notes_a_secret_name` | `op://` and `openshell:resolve:` values, secret note | none | S2, ER1 to ER3, ER5, ER7 |
| `jackin_fixed_workspace_env_keeps_the_role_scope_live_and_refuses_the_sources` | workspace env: role scope stays live, `p` refuses | none | S1, ES3, ES5 |
| `every_fixed_text_fits_the_surface_that_shows_it` | reason and note widths | none | D1 |
| `a_credential_reference_is_refused_in_any_letter_case_after_leading_space` | the value rule | none | ER5 to ER7 |
| `only_a_name_with_a_secret_word_gets_the_note` | the name rule | none | ER4 |
| `a_trust_source_stays_until_the_next_key_and_a_refusal_expires` | sticky `o`, 5 s refusal | none | TR4 |
| `the_demo_settings_keep_every_control` | demo Settings unchanged | none | DM1 to DM3 |
| `the_demo_env_form_keeps_its_sources_and_saves_what_it_is_given` | demo env form unchanged | none | DM4 |
| `the_demo_env_form_requires_a_value` | the value input is required in the demo form | none | BF5 |
| `a_rejected_rename_leaves_the_original_variable_alone`, `the_demo_env_form_stays_open_on_a_rejected_rename_and_changes_nothing` | a rejected rename stages nothing; the form stays open on Save | none | BF6, BF8 |
| `the_demo_env_form_saves_a_plain_value_that_starts_with_op` | demo accepts `op://` as plain | none | ER8 |

What the records show and what they cannot:

- A sticky status cannot be told from a 5 s status in a PTY test: the harness
  steps the clock with a paste, and a paste clears a sticky status. The library
  data test `a_trust_source_stays_until_the_next_key_and_a_refusal_expires`
  drives the real `App` with ticks instead (TR4).
- The reason texts are read from `Fixed::openshell()` by the tests, so a change
  of text only turns the frozen baselines red (TX1 to TX6). The two texts that
  no baseline shows (`enable_workspace`, `last_role`) stay unpinned, as the B4a
  review found. The refusal texts of the form (`env_secret_note`,
  `env_reference`) are asserted by the tests through the same data.
- The hardware cursor column of a baseline depends on the last frame that wrote
  to the terminal. `jackin_fixed_env_form` is captured after a frame that only
  moves the gutter, because the frame right after Tab left the edited Key input
  gave column 48 on a quiet machine and 80 when the machine was loaded.

## B4a fix round: the UX and red/green review findings

Reviews: `P9.3-B4a-ux.md` (CHANGES_REQUIRED) and `P9.3-B4a-redgreen.md`
(CHANGES_REQUIRED). The fixes are in commits `b53a4f9` (UX) and `4d68656`
(tests). Run them with:

```sh
cargo nextest run --locked -p jackin-console --test functional -E 'test(jackin_fixed_editor) | test(jackin_fixed_dirty_exit) | test(jackin_fixed_project_row) | test(jackin_fixed_workspace_account) | test(jackin_fixed_last_allowed_role)'
```

| Finding | Fix | Test or baseline | Break |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| UX F1 | the Project row no longer advertises `o Open source` | `jackin_fixed_project_row_keeps_its_paint_and_refuses_every_edit`, `jackin_fixed_editor_project` (row 39 only) | FX1 |
| UX F2 | `fixed()` enables every account | `jackin_fixed_editor_accounts` (row 9 only) | FX2 |
| UX D1, D2 | `last_role` is `Keep one role allowed · allow another first`; `enable_workspace` is `All accounts are active here · press p to prefer one` | none shows them in a frame | none, see below |
| RG row 3 | the footer of the focused fixed select has no `Space Toggle` | `jackin_fixed_dirty_exit_refuses_the_list_by_key_and_click` | FX3 |
| RG row 5 | the key loop adds `n` and `delete`; the three contrast assertions are deleted, the Add row assertion stays | `jackin_fixed_project_row_keeps_its_paint_and_refuses_every_edit` | FX4, FX5 |
| RG row 7 | a stale role name next to `backend` does not count as an allowed role | `jackin_fixed_last_allowed_role_cannot_be_unchecked` (the separate test was merged into it in the B4b fix round) | FX6 |
| UX F3 | a click on the last allowed role is refused | `jackin_fixed_last_allowed_role_refuses_a_click` | FX7 |

The two new tests are plan rows added in the fix round, for test-reviewer
judgement: the stale name is the T26 case "a name in the list that is no longer
a configured role counts as not allowed" (`editor_refuses_last_allowed_role`),
and the click is T21 assertion 2 for the last-role refusal.

Not covered by a break:

- The two reworded reasons. The tests read them from `Fixed::openshell()` and no
  frame shows them, so a typo in either is not caught here (reviewer D5, D6b).
- The three deleted contrast assertions. The reviewer showed that no break ever
  turned them red and that the frozen `jackin_fixed_editor_project` baseline
  catches a dim row (F1 to F10 of the review).
- Not done, left for later steps: UX F4 (post the reason on the first click of a
  fixed row), UX D3 (muted ink for L1 reasons), the Project row context menu
  items that only refuse, and the sticky-status gap of the B4a record.

## B4b fix round: the UX and red/green review findings

Reviews: `P9.3-B4b-ux.md` (CHANGES_REQUIRED, six required changes) and
`P9.3-B4b-redgreen.md` (CHANGES_REQUIRED, four required changes). Decision
D27 holds: no approved baseline changes. The three `jackin_fixed_*` baselines
that move are new in this unmerged branch, and
`git diff --name-status 00031c0..HEAD -- '*baselines*'` still lists only `A`
lines. Run the tests with:

```sh
cargo nextest run --locked -p jackin-console --test functional -E 'test(jackin_fixed)'
cargo nextest run --locked -p jackin-console --test fixed_settings --test vocabulary
```

| Finding | Fix | Test or baseline | Break |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| UX F1 | the reason under the source radio is `Tone::Muted` | `jackin_fixed_env_form` (row 17), contrast assertion in `jackin_fixed_env_sources_refuse_the_radio_options_and_the_p_key` | BF4 |
| UX F2 | the 1Password option stays visible and fixed, labelled `1Password item / field` (`Vocabulary.env_op_label`; the demo keeps its text) | `jackin_fixed_env_form` (row 14), the env sources test, `the_openshell_vocabulary_renames_the_demo_words` | BF3 |
| UX F3 | `env_reference` is `Plain values only · use an account for credentials`; the test waits for the error row `! <text>` because the same text is already on screen as the source note | `jackin_fixed_env_form_refuses_a_credential_reference_and_notes_a_secret_name` | BF7 |
| UX F4 | the Trust hint is `o Show source` (`Vocabulary.trust_hint`; the demo keeps `o Open source`) | `jackin_fixed_settings_trust` (row 39), `the_demo_settings_keep_every_control` | BF1, BF2 |
| UX F5 | the value input is required: `Value for the key *`, `Required` on an empty Save | `jackin_fixed_env_form` (row 19), `the_demo_env_form_requires_a_value` | BF5 |
| UX F6 | `save_env` checks for a duplicate before it touches `pending`; the unreachable `cfg.env` `Form` arm is deleted | `a_rejected_rename_leaves_the_original_variable_alone`, `the_demo_env_form_stays_open_on_a_rejected_rename_and_changes_nothing` | BF6 |
| Keep-open | recorded in `docs/current-state.md` as a deliberate demo change on the error path | the two rename tests and the reference test | BF8 |
| UX D1 | `env_sources` and `env_reference` are one string; the first secret note line is `Keep secrets out of env. Values are stored as plain text.` | `jackin_fixed_env_form` (row 17), `every_fixed_text_fits_the_surface_that_shows_it` | BF10, BF11 |
| UX D2 | the Agents detail says `no credentials handed to the sandbox` (`Vocabulary.agent_no_credentials`; the demo keeps `container`) | `jackin_fixed_settings_agents` (rows 8, 10 to 13) | BF9 |
| RG row 5 | the role overrides test waits for the `Scope` field and its value row | `jackin_fixed_settings_role_overrides_refuse_and_the_scope_lists_global` | BF12 |
| RG row 7 | the credential reference test waits for both note lines in one predicate | `jackin_fixed_env_form_refuses_a_credential_reference_and_notes_a_secret_name` | BF13 |
| RG row 4 | the agents test presses `c` and waits for the Accounts page | `jackin_fixed_settings_agents_refuse_every_row` | BF14 |
| RG row 21 | `jackin_fixed_last_allowed_role_cannot_be_unchecked` shows `ghost-role` checked and `not in registry` before it moves on; the separate stale-name test is deleted | `jackin_fixed_last_allowed_role_cannot_be_unchecked` | BF15, BF16 (FX6) |

Re-accepted baselines, by cell diff against the committed file (text, colours
and attributes of every cell; the cursor is unchanged in all three):

| Baseline | Rows that change |
| --- | --- |
| `jackin_fixed_settings_trust` | 39: `o Open source` to `o Show source` |
| `jackin_fixed_env_form` | 14: the option label loses `  (recommended for secrets)`. 17: the note reads `Plain values only · use an account for credentials` in `8a8a8a` instead of `737373`. 19: `Value for the key  optional` to `Value for the key *` |
| `jackin_fixed_settings_agents` | 8, 10, 11, 12, 13: `container` to `sandbox` (the only ink change is on the two cells that the shorter word leaves blank) |

No approved baseline is touched. The value input is required in the demo form
too, which no approved baseline shows (the 15 approved baselines replay green).

What the records show and what they cannot:

- The torn-frame race of rows 5 and 7 cannot be turned red by a one-edit change
  to the code: the old waits failed once in 600 runs under load, because they
  read a half-drawn dialog. BF12 and BF13 show that the new waits are selective:
  they time out with a message that names the missing part. The proof of the
  race fix is the stress run: the six changed PTY tests, 30 iterations at
  `-j 4` (`cargo nextest run --locked -j 4 -p jackin-console --test functional
  --stress-count 30 -E '<the six tests>'`), `30/30 stress run iterations: 30
  passed`, exit 0.
- BF14 is the reviewer's break A8 and BF15 is FXD. Both stayed green against the
  old tests and are red against the fixed ones.
- The test `the_demo_env_form_keeps_an_empty_value_open_with_its_message` and
  its record DM5 are gone. With the input required, Save on an empty value stops
  at the form and never reaches `save_env`, so removing `keep_open_on_save` no
  longer turns that test red. The keep-open behaviour is pinned by the two
  rejected-rename tests and by the reference test (BF8). The
  `Value cannot be empty` check in `save_env` stays as the review asked, as a
  backstop that no key or click reaches.
- The assertion that the two secret note lines together fit 64 columns is
  deleted. It fitted the old single sentence; the lines are independent now and
  each is still held to 62 columns (BF10).
- The new wording of `env_secret_note[1]`, `env_sources` and `env_reference` is
  not pinned beyond the frozen frames: the tests read those texts from
  `Fixed::openshell()`. `env_secret_note` has no frame.
- BF16 is the former FX6, rerun against the merged test. ER1 was rerun and ER2 is
  the break of BF8; both now quote the new error row text. TX5 and the older records that quote texts of
  earlier rounds are history.

## B1b: packaging, one app and two modes (BX22, G1 to G5)

Tests: `apps/jackin/tests/product_mode.rs`, `product_manifest.rs`,
`library_has_no_demo.rs`. Plan: test plan v7.1 delta T34 BL, with the
review amendments N1 and N2 (and A1 to A5 of the v7 review).

Differences from the plan text, each decided while building:

- `product_manifest` has two tests, not five (N2 preferred). `manifest_shape`
  collects every broken rule and prints all of them; each plausible edit of
  the manifest is one record below, and each makes it red. The second test,
  `product_mode_test_is_gated_on_the_demo_being_off`, is N1: it reads the
  first line of `tests/product_mode.rs`.
- `product_mode` has two tests. `assemble`, `Entry` and `AppOptions` do not
  exist before B2c, so the test builds the app with `App::for_scenario`, and
  the test `Backend` and `OnePassword` implement the full trait (about 90
  lines, not 30). The methods the manager never calls are `unreachable!`.
  The second test covers the About rows from `Surfaces.about`.
- `library_has_no_demo` scans an in-memory list of `(path, text)` pairs
  (`scan(&[Source], pending)`), so the fixture trees need no temporary
  directory. The real-tree test reads `apps/jackin/src`. `demo_modules_are_cfg_gated`
  reads the real `lib.rs`; its negative cases are the separate test
  `an_ungated_demo_module_is_reported`.
- The `1Password locked: unlock the app and retry` literal is scoped to
  `launch.rs`, because `accounts.rs` and `backend.rs` carry the same
  sentence as real domain text.
- The breaks of the scanner tests change the scanner in the test file
  itself (it is the code under test); the other breaks change library
  source or the manifest.

Reproduce one record (the product-shape tests use
`--no-default-features --features product` instead of `--all-features`):

```sh
cargo nextest run --locked --no-fail-fast -p jackin-console --all-features \
  --test <file> -E 'test(=<name>)'
```

### B1b evidence (shell commands, not tests)

Test counts, `cargo nextest list --locked --workspace --all-features`:
282 before B1b, 293 after (exit 0). `diff` of the sorted lists shows 11
added names and 0 removed: 9 in `library_has_no_demo`, 2 in
`product_manifest`. `product_mode` is gated off under `--all-features`.

Product shape, `cargo nextest list --locked -p jackin-console
--no-default-features --features product`: 13 tests, 9 `library_has_no_demo`,
2 `product_manifest`, 2 `product_mode` (the `cfg` line of `product_mode.rs`
is therefore live; `product_mode_test_is_gated_on_the_demo_being_off` keeps
it so).

The silently skipped suite: `cargo nextest list --locked --workspace`
(default features, no flag) lists 151 tests. The 144 `jackin-console`
tests that need the demo are absent from it, without a failure. This is why
every gate command names `--all-features`.

```text
$ cargo build --locked -p jackin-console --no-default-features
    Finished `dev` profile [unoptimized + debuginfo] target(s)   (exit 0)

$ cargo tree --locked -p jackin-console --no-default-features -e normal -i clap
error: package ID specification `clap` did not match any packages   (exit 101)

$ cargo tree --locked -p jackin-console --features demo -e normal -i clap
clap v4.6.7
├── jackin-console v0.1.0
└── junie-tui v0.1.0
```

lists four Rust jobs: `rust-jackin-console`, `rust-jackin-console-product`,

```text
stack/rust/root/*/default               the same four with --all-features
```

Job names in `ci.yml`: `Rust / jackin-console`, `Rust / jackin-console (product)`,
`Rust / junie-tui`, `Rust / junie-tui (product)`; `required` lists all four in
`needs`. `suite-functional` keeps `nextest run --workspace --no-tests fail
--profile functional` with no feature flag (suites have no feature field).

The first full demo run ended 292 passed, 1 failed:
`jackin::jackin_first_use_empty_manager_and_intro` timed out after 11.4 s
waiting for `Current directory` while the intro rain was still on screen.
The host load average was 73 (other sessions). Three solo reruns of that
test passed (12 to 13 s each), and the second full run passed 293 of 293.
No code of this step touches the intro; the failure is the 10 s observation
deadline under load.

## B2c: Entry and AppOptions replace Scenario (BX18, G6, G17)

Tests: `apps/jackin/tests/entry.rs` (six tests; the fix round adds two,
and `tests/clipboard.rs` in the root crate adds one). Plan: test plan v7.1 delta
T35, with review amendment N5 (the native sink wording is tested once, here,
and is deleted from T49). N17 (`console_fixture_args`) belongs to the jackin
step J1 and is not part of this step. The step moves `Scenario`, `CLOCK` and
`entry_for` into `fixtures`, so the `PENDING` list of `library_has_no_demo`
loses its `Scenario` and `CLOCK` rows; the two records at the end of this
section show the scanner rejecting both names again.

Differences from the plan text, each decided while building:

- `native_sink_decides_the_wording` and `clipboard_modes` first copied the
  container row of the capsule Debug info dialog (`ctrl+b i`, then `y`) and
  accepted `Copied 29 characters · jackin-payments-platform-7f3a` instead of
  `Copied selection`, on the claim that a drag over a script pane forms no
  selection. The claim was wrong: with a render between the events, as the
  runtime does, a drag on `CapsuleMulti` does form one (see "B2c fix round").
  The likely cause of the first result is events fed without a render, so no
  hit regions were registered (inferred, not checked). Both tests now drive
  the drag. The Preview status is asserted exactly.
- With `ClipboardMode::Preview` the plan says the sink gets nothing. A sink
  cannot be attached to `Preview`, so the test asserts what can differ: no
  OSC 52 string reaches `Application::take_output`.
- `start_table_matches_the_old_scenarios` has 19 rows for the 15 scenarios
  (`first-use` twice, the launch and outro scenarios at frames 0 and 30). The
  expected routes and texts were checked against the commit before this step:
  a throwaway test printed route, screen length and byte sum of the
  140 by 40 screen for all 15 scenarios at frames 0 and 30 on both trees, and
  the 30 lines are identical.
- `hard_cases_credentials_plan` drives the manager launch picker with real
  keys under `Motion::Full` and ticks (150) until the credentials dialog
  appears. Its negatives are a signed-in 1Password session and the
  `Claude · Personal` row.
- `LaunchEntry` is a type alias of `LaunchTarget` (the argument of
  `Backend::launch_plan`); the two designed structs have the same four
  fields. `NativeClipboard` lives in `junie_tui::shell`, where
  `Clipboard::copy` is, not in `jackin_console`.
- `fixtures::app_with_world` exists next to `app_for` for tests that change
  the world before they build the app. `app_for` calls it, so there is one
  construction path.
- The command of the plan, `rg -w Scenario apps/jackin/src --glob
  '!fixtures/**' --glob '!bin/**'`, matches the globs against the working
  directory, so from the repository root it still lists `fixtures/`. Run from
  `apps/jackin/src`, or with `--glob '!**/fixtures/**'`, it has 0 hits.
- Two existing tests changed beyond the mechanical edit.
  `snapshot::apply_leaves_the_console_state_alone` loses its
  `target.scenario` line, and
  `world::every_scenario_builds_a_world_that_names_it` reads the About row
  `Scenario`, because `World.scenario` no longer exists.

Reproduce one record:

```sh
cargo nextest run --locked --no-fail-fast -p jackin-console --all-features \
  --test <file> -E 'test(=<name>)'
```

The `real_tree_is_clean` records use `--no-default-features --features
product` instead of `--all-features`. D3 first ran with `is_none_or` in
place of `is_some_and`; that run stayed green because no row of the test has
an absent account, so the break was replaced by `is_some()`, which goes red.

## B2c fix round (review P9.3-B2c-redgreen, F1 to F4)

Review verdict CHANGES_REQUIRED on B2c. Every record below was made on the
fixed tree with `-j 4`: apply the break, run the command, restore the file.
Each break is red on the test named and green again on the restored file.

Tests added or changed:

- F1: `entry::clipboard_modes` and `entry::native_sink_decides_the_wording`
  drive a real drag on `CapsuleMulti` (render, Down at x 12, Drag at x 30 and
  50, Up, row 7, rendering after each event). Accepting sink: status
  `Copied selection`, sink text `the settlement retry loop so failed ba`, OSC
  52 string for that text. Refusing sink: status `Copy requested · this
  terminal may ignore OSC 52`. Preview: no OSC 52 output and the exact status
  `Copied selection`, plus the exact Debug info row status in Preview. The wording test keeps the Debug info row for the
  `Copied 29 characters · ...` branch.
- F2: `tests/clipboard.rs::a_native_write_overrides_an_osc52_refusal`, through
  `Clipboard::new().limit(8)` and `.osc52(false)` with `.native(sink)`.
- F3: `entry::roles_page_selects_the_named_role`, `EntryPage::Roles { select:
  Some("the-architect") }` through `assemble`.
- F4: `entry::launch_failure_plan_fails_the_network_stage`, `app(LaunchFailure)`
  at frame 10000 under `Motion::Paused`.

```sh
cargo nextest run --locked -j 4 --test clipboard
```

## B3b: Surfaces::openshell, the live-data constructors and the literal sweep (BX19, BX20, G7, G8, G9)

Tests: `apps/jackin/tests/surfaces.rs` (five tests, test plan v7.1 delta
T37 BL, no review amendment applies to them), plus the four scanner gaps
that the B1b red and green review listed
(`P9.3-B1b-redgreen.md`, Suggestions 1, 2, 4 and 5). The scanner gaps add
`demo_modules_are_not_reachable_through_an_alias` and
`a_pending_row_counts_occurrences` to `library_has_no_demo.rs` and
`only_the_demo_may_enable_clap` to `product_manifest.rs`. Two tests of
`tests/vocabulary.rs` are edited as in design 4.5 (F26), and a third that
pinned the old word is edited the same way.

Differences from the plan text, each decided while building:

- The normalizers (`domain::openshell`) and `domain::fixed_fields` of the
  design row BX20 are not built here. The brief for this step lists the
  live constructors, the stage list and the sweep; the tie test that would
  use them (T36) is a jackin step (J1). One input needs a decision first:
  the design classes `Account.origin` as Fixed (`Registered`), but the
  `openshell-fixed` world holds `Discovered` accounts that the approved
  baseline `jackin_fixed_editor_accounts` shows, so a normalizer that sets
  `origin` would move a baseline (D27).
- `Surfaces` has one field more than design 4.4 counts: `cockpit_version:
  Option<String>`. The cockpit Debug info shows `0.6.4 · preview` and the
  capsule Debug info shows `0.9.2` in the same demo, so one `version`
  cannot serve both. The demo sets `cockpit_version`; the live value is
  `None` and the cockpit falls back to `version`.
- `Vocabulary::default()` moved to `fixtures` as `Vocabulary::demo()`, not
  only `Surfaces::default()`. The new demo texts hold literals the scanner
  rejects in `domain/` (`payments-platform`, `/Users/alexey/`,
  `Preview clipboard is empty`), so the demo words cannot stay in the
  library. `AccountForm::default()` stays: it holds no scanned literal.
- The field for the `spawning` status is named `pane_starting`. The scanner
  matches string literals, and the field name in `Vocabulary::fields()` is
  one.
- The scanner no longer has rules for the dialog titles `Close tab?` and
  `Close pane?`. Design 4.5 gives the live title `Close pane?` too, so the
  title is not demo text; the bodies (`Reap ...`) stay rules.
- `Paste clipboard` and `opened on host` are new scanner rules. The second
  is the `Opened`-class false string on the export path, next to
  `revealed in Finder`; it is a `Vocabulary` field with `None` live.
- Data-driven labels dispatch by a guard on the data label
  (`l if l == w.surfaces.vocabulary.about_label`), the form that
  `info_label()` already uses, not by a `CapsuleCmd` enum. The literal
  labels keep their match arms.
- `Account.launch_blocked` is `Option<LaunchBlock>`. `usability_of` returns
  the new `Usability::Blocked(LaunchBlock)` and `Usability::label` takes
  `Option<&Fixed>`; with no `Fixed` the label is `unavailable`. The five
  call sites pass `w.surfaces.fixed.as_ref()`. `tests/account_data.rs`
  sets `Fixed` and the enum instead of the text.
- `world::Takeover { at_ms, by }` replaces `takeover_at_ms: Option<i64>`
  (the field name stays so its `PENDING` row stays until B6); the demo
  scenario `hard-cases` sets `by` in `fixtures`.
- The live texts that design 4.5 gives only as a field are mine; each
  differs from the demo text and passes the false-string test:
  `Nothing copied in this console yet` (empty clipboard),
  `{label}: not available`, `Not available` (palette detail),
  `Exported to ~/Downloads/jackin/{workspace}/{path}` (from the UX
  critique), no reveal and no open note, no host-log row, no config path
  (`workspace config`, `global config`), `The workspace config could not
  be written.` and `The global config could not be written.`
- The close dialog chooses its body from the focused pane's
  `proc.agent` (agent or shell) now; B8b swaps that for `PaneBody::kind()`.
- Not observable through the app, so covered by the scanner and by the
  vocabulary field test only: the `spawning` status (the same call replaces
  it with `Started ...`), the two `not available` fallbacks (only a
  disabled row reaches them), the two save-failure dialogs (observable
  after all: the `hard-cases` scenario fails the first save, see "B3b UX
  fixes") and the cockpit Debug info row (the key does not open
  the dialog under the in-process harness at frame 10000).
- The accounts sentence `1Password locked: unlock the app and retry` in the
  simulated refresh (`accounts.rs:447`) is now `OpError::Locked.message()`
  and the scanner rule covers `screens/accounts.rs` (gap 4). The shorter
  `failed · 1Password locked` outcome (`accounts.rs:450`) is the simulated
  refresh and moves in B6; `1Password locked` at `accounts.rs:2096` is the
  label of a real issue code.
- `tests/product_mode.rs` builds its world with `Surfaces::openshell`, so
  the product shape renders the live words.
- `Scope::OnlyIn` takes a list of paths and matches the whole path.

Reproduce one record:

```sh
cargo nextest run --locked --no-fail-fast -p jackin-console --all-features \
  --test <file> -E 'test(=<name>)'
```

The records for `surfaces_openshell_sets_every_field` use `--no-default-features
--features product`, so they also show the test runs in the product job; the
tests in the `demo` module are named `demo::<name>`.

### B3b evidence (shell commands, not tests)

Test counts, `cargo nextest list --locked --workspace --all-features`: 299
before B3b, 307 after (`surfaces` 5, `library_has_no_demo` 2 and
`product_manifest` 1 are new). The product shape,
`cargo nextest list --locked -p jackin-console --no-default-features
--features product`: 13 before, 17 after (`surfaces_openshell_sets_every_field`
is the one new test of `surfaces` that does not need the demo).

Visual identity: `git status --porcelain -- tests/functional/baselines
apps/jackin/tests/functional/baselines` prints nothing after the full demo
run, and every functional journey ends in its frozen `App::visual` check,
so each approved baseline matched. The OpenShell launch composition is
covered by `jackin_launch_openshell`.

## B3b UX fixes (F1 to F4, the config path, `Not supported`, the Manager word)

Plan: the B3b UX review (CHANGES_REQUIRED, F1 to F4) and the orchestrator's
decisions on it: F1 to F4 as written; the real config path through
`LiveFacts`; `Not supported` on the `Stage image…` rows (F7); the Manager
says `Capsule` in the OpenShell set (F8); the export reveal and open rows
stay for B4c (F5). Every new live string is a `Vocabulary` or `Surfaces`
field whose demo value is the old text.

Tests: `apps/jackin/tests/live_texts.rs` (9 new tests); changed
`tests/vocabulary.rs` (the Manager words, the Debug info row, the banned
word `Capsule` now allows `daemon.*`), `tests/surfaces.rs` (`Not
supported`, `config_path`), `tests/library_has_no_demo.rs` (five new
literal rules) and `tests/product_mode.rs` (`LiveFacts`). Commits: `8d28569`
(the fixes), `6a996c1` (the fixed world's config path). Every break below
was applied to a copy of the tree at `ac0eb89` and reverted.

New fields and live values (`Vocabulary::openshell()` unless noted; the
demo value is the old text in `Vocabulary::demo()`):

| Field | Demo (unchanged) | Live |
|---|---|---|
| `paste_empty_reason` (F1) | `The clipboard is empty` | `Nothing copied yet` |
| `save_error_workspace`, `save_error_global` (F2) | `write failed: ~/.jackin/... is not writable (EACCES)` | `Could not write {config}.` |
| `save_error_next` (F2) | `fix the permission and Save again` | `check disk space and permissions, then Save again` |
| `sandbox_id`, `invocation_id` (F3) | `Some("3f9c{run8}e21a")`, `Some("{run}-4f11")` | `None` (the rows are absent) |
| `attached_status` (F4) | `Attached to {name} · tabs and panes restored` | `Attached to {name}` |
| `workspace_config`, `global_config` (F6) | `{home}/.jackin/workspaces/{name}.toml`, `{home}/.jackin/config.toml` | `{config}` (rows read `workspace config · ~/.config/jackin/config.toml`) |
| `command_unavailable`, `detail_unavailable` (F7) | `{label}: not available in the preview`, `Not in this preview` | `{label}: not supported`, `Not supported` |
| `daemon.inspect_label`, `no_tabs`, `unavailable_stopped`, `list_tabs`, `list_no_tabs` (F8) | `Daemon`, `Daemon reports no tabs`, ... | `Capsule`, `No Capsule tabs open`, `No live Capsule (sandbox not running)`, `capsule · {tabs} · {panes}`, `capsule · no tabs` |

Differences from the review text:

- The `Error` row of the save-failure dialog says `Could not write
  {config}.`, not `jackin could not write the workspace config.`. The
  orchestrator asked the dialog to name the file. The row holds 52 columns
  (the demo text is cut with `…` at the same place), and `jackin could not
  write ~/.config/jackin/config.toml.` is 53, so the rendered row lost the
  end of the file name. `Could not write ~/.config/jackin/config.toml.` is
  45 columns. The workspace and the global dialog now read the same; the
  title (`Save failed · <name>`, `Settings error`) tells them apart.
- The path is `Surfaces.config_path` (a 13th `Surfaces` field, Data) set by
  `LiveFacts.config_path`. `Vocabulary::openshell()` stays a constant, so
  the tie of the fixed world still compares it. `World::config_file` shows
  the path with `~`. `openshell-fixed` sets
  `<home>/.config/jackin/config.toml`; the demo leaves it `None` and its
  templates ignore it.
- The earlier claim in this file that the save-failure dialogs are not
  observable (the B3b notes: "the demo has no failing save") was wrong.
  `SimBackend::hard_cases` fails the first save, and the tests drive both
  dialogs on the `hard-cases` scenario (`down e ] enter r i ctrl+s` in the
  editor, `s enter space ctrl+s` in Settings, then Save and ticks).
- `command_unavailable` is still unreachable through the app: only a
  disabled row reaches it, and a disabled row does not run. The live value is
  pinned by `unsupported_rows_say_not_supported` as data.
- The pane-menu reason is checked on the right-click menu of the
  `capsule-multi` scenario (`Secondary` mouse event, then a hover on the
  `Paste` row), the menu border shows it.
- Not changed, on purpose: the `Export file and reveal`, `Export file and
  open` rows and their status texts (B4c, F5).

## B3b-1: the Fixed-field extractors and their tests

Plan: the origin decision (b), sections 6.1 to 6.4, as amended by the T36
[BL] review (A1 to A9). Tests: `apps/jackin/tests/fixed_fields.rs`
(`#![cfg(feature = "demo")]`, so it runs in the demo suite only, like
`tests/entry.rs`). Commit `d6ec97b` carries the extractors and the tests that
hold on the fixture of that time; the account, role, instance and surface
checks of the fixed world carry the fixture change and are in `7b2339c`
(B3b-2), because a test that is red on its parent commit may not be
committed before the fix. Every test name starts with `fixed_world`, so
`-E 'test(fixed_world)'` selects them all.

What the extractors cover is the table in
[current state](current-state.md#fixed-fields-of-the-openshell-world): every
record is destructured without `..` (`Workspace`, `AccountPolicy`, `Mount`,
`GlobalConfig`, `TrustRow`, `Account`, `RoleEntry`, `Instance`,
`AccountRegistry`, `Surfaces`, `World` with its 25 fields, `Snapshot` with
its 8), and `Console` is the class of a `World` field the running console
owns and projection never produces (`clock`, `backend`, `op` and the like).

Known residual hole (A8): a field that is wrong in the table and wrong in the
expected literal of a test passes every check. The destructuring forces a
class for each field and the tests pin each Fixed value with a literal, but
nothing derives the class from a second source. Only a review of the table
closes it; `Fixed::openshell()` is the second source (each of its reasons
names a Fixed control) and should be read against the table once.

Differences from the plan text:

- A1: one test per record instead of one test with sequential asserts:
  `fixed_world_workspaces`, `_global`, `_accounts`, `_roles`, `_instances`
  and `_surfaces`. The surface test has six `assert_eq!`, each with the
  field name as the message.
- A3: the negatives start from records built in the test (and assert the
  starting record equals the Fixed value first), one case per field. The
  coupled pair `disabled_defaults` and `enabled_is_every_account` is one case
  that expects both flags.
- A4: the expected surface value is built from the six owned constructors
  (`Vocabulary::openshell()`, `Fixed::openshell()`, `AccountForm::openshell()`,
  `AgentBlocked::openshell()`, `WizardDefaults::openshell()`,
  `LaunchRoleRule::Configured`), not from `SurfaceFixed::openshell()`. The
  truth of the six constants is T37's; this row proves the fixed world
  carries them.
- A5: the positive tests assert 4 workspaces, 12 accounts, 4 trust rows and
  `agent_modes.len() == Agent::ALL.len()`. `non_launchable_modes_ignore` is
  "every agent of `Agent::ALL` except the launchable one is present and
  `Ignore`", so an empty map or a missing agent is `false`; the extractor
  test removes the second agent and clears the map.
- A6: `RoleEntry.trusted` and `Instance.workdir` are Fixed (orchestrator
  decision); `AccountPolicy`, `TrustRow`, `Mount`, `RoleEntry`, `Instance`,
  `AccountRegistry` (`revision` is Data), `World` and `Snapshot` are
  destructured. `instances`, `github` and `index_error` are Data.
- `Surfaces` has 13 fields (`cockpit_version` and `config_path` came after the
  design's 11).
- The three `World` fields the destructure names (`refresh_fails`,
  `takeover_at_ms`, `next_workspace_id`) are demo tokens the scanner knows;
  `library_has_no_demo` has three new B6 rows for `domain/fixed_fields.rs`,
  and B6 deletes them with the fields.

### R0: the fixed-world tests against the unmodified fixture

The tests of the fixed world were written and run before the normalizers
existed, so `fixtures::surfaces::fixed` was still the version of `f54ca1b`
(the extractors of `d6ec97b` and the five tests below, uncommitted):

```sh
cargo nextest run --locked --no-fail-fast -p jackin-console --all-features \
  --test fixed_fields -E 'test(fixed_world)'
```

Red (exit 100). Each failure names the record or the field:

```text
fixed_world_accounts      (tests/fixed_fields.rs:146)  assertion `left == right` failed: disc-amp
  left: AccountFixed { enabled: true, origin: Discovered }
  right: AccountFixed { enabled: true, origin: Registered }
fixed_world_roles         (tests/fixed_fields.rs:155)  assertion `left == right` failed: acme-labs/data-eng
  left: RoleFixed { trusted: false }
  right: RoleFixed { trusted: true }
fixed_world_instances     (tests/fixed_fields.rs:170)  assertion `left == right` failed: jk-7f3a
  left: InstanceFixed { workdir: "/workspace/payments-platform" }
  right: InstanceFixed { workdir: "/sandbox/project" }
fixed_world_surfaces      (tests/fixed_fields.rs:187)  assertion `left == right` failed: account_form
  left: AccountForm { providers: [ProviderChoice { provider: Anthropic, label: "Claude Code · Anthropic / Claude" }, ...
fixed_world_views_compose_and_agree (tests/fixed_fields.rs:202)  assertion `left == right` failed
  left: [AccountFixed { enabled: true, origin: Registered }, ...
Summary 12 tests run: 7 passed, 5 failed, 0 skipped
```

The test later renamed `fixed_world_view_is_the_openshell_constants` was
`fixed_world_views_compose_and_agree` then. `fixed_world_workspaces` and
`fixed_world_global` passed: the old `fixed()` already set those fields. The
surface test stops at the first wrong field (`account_form`; `vocabulary` and
`fixed` were already right).

Green (exit 0), on `ac0eb89`, after the normalizers:

```text
Summary 20 tests run: 20 passed, 0 skipped
```

### Extractor breaks

Each break makes one extractor ignore its field (a constant equal to the
Fixed value replaces the read), so the positive tests stay green and only the
negative case of that field can fail. The M1 and compile-gate records are
compile reds (`E0560`, `E0027`); they prove the type gate and count as
type-gate reds only, never as runtime reds.

#### `fixed_world_extractor_sees_every_workspace_field`: workspace_fixed ignores `workdir`

Break in `apps/jackin/src/domain/fixed_fields.rs`:

```diff
-        workdir: workdir.clone(),
-        mounts:
+        workdir: "/sandbox/project".into(),
+        mounts:
```

## B3b-2: the normalizers and the fixed world

Plan: the origin decision, sections 6.2 and 6.3, and the amendments A2, A7
and A9. Code: `domain/openshell.rs` (`account`, `workspace`, `global`,
`role`, `instance`, `surfaces`, `PROJECT_DIR`), `Surfaces::openshell` through
`SurfaceFixed::openshell()`, `fixtures::surfaces::fixed` rewritten to call the
normalizers. Commit `7b2339c`. Tests: the rest of
`apps/jackin/tests/fixed_fields.rs` (the six `fixed_world_*` checks of the
fixed world, `fixed_world_view_is_the_openshell_constants`, five
`fixed_world_normalizer_*` tests, `fixed_world_normalizers_leave_data_fields_alone`,
`fixed_world_normalizers_are_idempotent`); `ac0eb89` adds the provenance and
name assertions of `fixed_world_accounts`.

Differences from the plan text:

- Six normalizers, not four: `role` and `instance` are the single definition
  of the two Fixed fields the orchestrator added (`RoleEntry.trusted`,
  `Instance.workdir`).
- A2: the normalizer tests start from wrong records (`Workspace::new` already
  has `git_pull` on; the test adds `keep_awake`, `Discard`, two mounts, a
  disabled default, a role preference). K3 (`dirty_policy` left unset) turns
  only the normalizer test red: `fixed_world_workspaces` stays green because
  no workspace of the demo world holds another policy, which is why A2 exists.
- A7: the Data test destructures every record without `..` and gives every
  Data field a probe value (21 `Account` fields, 18 `Instance` fields, the
  13 `Surfaces` fields). Two exceptions are stated in the test: the mount
  source equals the `host_path` argument, and `accounts.enabled` is Fixed
  through the predicate, so the normalizer writes that set.
- The fixed world holds no `Discovered` account. The five former ones are
  `Registered`; four are named `host login`, `ci-bot` stays, and the
  provenance `LiveHost` became `ConfiguredSource`. Setting only
  `origin = Registered` moved `jackin_fixed_editor_accounts`
  (the label `host login` comes from a `Discovered` account named
  `discovered`); the rename keeps the baseline byte for byte, and the record
  of the fixture break below shows it.
- The fixed world takes the live `account_form`, `agent_blocked`,
  `launch_role` and `wizard`, trusts every role (`acme-labs/data-eng` was
  untrusted) and sets every instance workdir to `/sandbox/project` (it was
  `/workspace/<name>`). No approved baseline moved.
- Not covered by a test: the S1 and S2 reds assume the expected surface value
  is built from the six constructors (A4); both are red below.

## B3b red/green fixes

Plan: the independent B3b red/green review (CHANGES_REQUIRED, findings 1 to

No production code and no baseline moves. The reds are the reviewer's own
breaks (R3a, R2a, R2b, A7a, A7b, A7c), re-run here against the fixed tests.
Each break is applied to the production file, run with
`cargo nextest run --locked --no-fail-fast -j 4 -p jackin-console --all-features -E 'test(fixed_world)'`,
then reverted and run again. All breaks were reverted: `git status --porcelain`
shows only the test file.

fixed head, 21 `fixed_world` tests pass.

### Finding 1: `all_trusted` has a two-row negative

`global_fixed` computes `all_trusted` over every trust row. The one-row case
cannot tell `.all` from `.any`. `fixed_world_extractor_sees_every_global_field`
gets two cases: a trusted row followed by an untrusted one, and an untrusted
row followed by a trusted one. The new helper `trust_row(trusted)` builds the
rows.

```diff
+        all_trusted: trust.iter().any(|row| {
```

## PTY torn frames

Decision D27 holds: no approved baseline changes. `git status --porcelain` on
`tests/functional/baselines` and `apps/jackin/tests/functional/baselines` is
empty before and after the work.

Symptom: under machine load the full suite fails a different PTY test on almost
every run, and each test passes alone. Cases in the brief:
`jackin_fixed_project_row_keeps_its_paint_and_refuses_every_edit`
(`expected one physical-row match for "~/src/payments-platform", got []`, 0.59 s,
frame with an empty Source column), `jackin_account_required_masked_save_cancel_and_remove`
(`filter has no remaining matching account`, the list under the header still
held the unfiltered accounts) and `jackin_first_use_empty_manager_and_intro`, plus the
two B4b fixes (role overrides, credential reference) that were made one wait at
a time.

### First wrong state

Reproduced with a temporary probe test, removed before the commit. It started
`jackin-preview --scenario openshell-fixed` at 120x40, opened the Project tab as
the test does (`down`, `e`, `]`, `enter`), polled `capture_snapshot` in a tight
loop for 1.2 s, and kept every distinct snapshot with its time. It also recorded
the raw PTY reads with `Operation::StartRecording` in the asciicast format
(one `o` event per `read` of the harness reader thread).

Snapshot sequence of one torn run (microseconds after `]`):

```text
 594  Project tab not drawn yet
1329  "Destination" present, "~/src/payments-platform" absent   <- the frame of the failed test
1692  complete frame
2054  complete frame with the cursor on the Project row
```

The frame at 1329 us is the frame in the log of the failure: header
`Destination ... Source`, then `› /sandbox/project  rw  clone  host` and nothing
after `host`, while the rows under it (footer, hints) are still the old frame's
cells because the diff does not resend unchanged cells.

Raw reads of the same run (sizes in bytes, tail and head of each read):

```text
2.0 ms  1023  \x1b[4;3H\x1b[38;2;179;179;179;48;2;0;0;0m General ...   ...host\x1b[38;2;138;138;138;48;2;23;63;28m  \x1b[38;2;
2.2 ms   979  255;255;255;48;2;23;63;28m  \x1b[38;2;179;179;179;48;2;23;63;28m~/src/pay ...   \x1b[39m\x1b[49m\x1b[59m\x1b[0m\x1b[?25l
2.7 ms   460  \x1b[4;14H ...                                                        (the frame for `enter`)
```

The first read ends inside an SGR sequence, one cell before the Source text. The
reader thread feeds every read to the emulator under the state lock
(`tuiscotti-tui-test-0.2.1/src/session.rs:207`), so a snapshot taken between two
reads shows half of a frame. The torn probe runs I read (four of them) split at the same byte (a first read of 1024
bytes, the next of 1023 or 978). The Rust `Stdout` is a
`LineWriter` with a 1024-byte buffer, and ratatui's `flush` of a 2.4 KB frame
reaches the PTY as several `write(2)` calls. The first frame of the process is
330 KB, so a long run of writes. A terminal has no frame boundary, so the PTY
delivers whatever the app has written so far.

Counts of snapshots that show "Destination" without the Source text, 10 burners,
machine load average 8 to 140:

| Tree | Runs | Torn |
| --- | --- | --- |
| before, HEAD `8978130` | 5 + 30 + 150 | 1 + 3 + 8 = 12 |
| after, `draw_frame` | 40 + 150 | 0 + 0 = 0 |

A second recorded failure of the class, from the unmodified tree
(`showcase_wheel_scrolls_pointed_grid_without_moving_sidebar`, load 120): the
test waits for the main-pane text `Northwind Traders`, then reads the sidebar of
that snapshot as the reference. The reference lacked the rows `Text & code` and
`Screens`, the later snapshot had them. The account-remove failure above is the
same shape: the wait is for the list header, the assertion is about the list
rows drawn after it.

### Why the structure allowed it

Every PTY test treats one `capture_snapshot` as one frame. Nothing in the
product marked a frame, so a test that waits for one string and asserts another
string of the same frame has a window the size of one `write(2)` gap. Under
load that gap is a descheduled app thread, and the probe hit the window in 12 of 185 runs
at its polling rate. B4b removed single windows by waiting for
the other string too. That works per test and does not survive the next test.

The harness emulator is alacritty (`vte` 0.15). Its parser implements DEC 2026
synchronized updates: after `CSI ? 2026 h` it buffers bytes until `CSI ? 2026 l`
(`vte-0.15.0/src/ansi.rs:298`). The product never sent either sequence
(`runtime.rs` had four `terminal.draw` calls and no bracket), so the emulator
could not know where a frame ended. The sequence is the standard way for a
terminal program to say so, and real terminals that support it (Ghostty, kitty,
iTerm2, WezTerm, Alacritty, tmux) tear in the same way without it.

This is a product fault and not only a test fault: a real terminal without
synchronized output shows the same half frame. The fix is in the shared runtime,
per the component rules.

Related sites of the same structure:

- `src/runtime.rs`, the four `terminal.draw` calls in `event_loop`.
- 15 `wait_screen` predicates call `find` or `row_with`, which assert and
  panic on a missing needle (11 in `jackin.rs`, 4 in `showcase.rs`). With whole
  frames only, such a predicate can still see a complete earlier frame without
  the row. None failed in the runs below. They are listed as a suggestion.
- The `tablepro_write_gate` `cursor.col` mismatch has another cause (see "Not
  fixed" below): the frame is whole, and the order of two frames differs.

### A second cause

`showcase_keyboard_activation_after_click_focus` failed once in the 10 baseline
runs (`timed out: keyboard activation fires the focused control`). The test
clicks `Preview`, so the status line already reads `Preview ✓`, then presses
`tab` and `enter`, which moves focus to `Cancel` and activates it. The wait
accepted `Run task ✓` or `Preview ✓`. It passed on the status of the click, before the
keys were handled, and the snapshot of the checkpoint `002` of every green run
shows `Preview ✓` in the green runs I read. When the app handled both keys before the first poll, the
status was `Cancel ✓` and the wait never matched. It is a stale-state wait, not a torn frame.

### Third cause, a time budget

`jackin_first_use_empty_manager_and_intro` plays the real-time intro. The key
`space` does nothing there (`apps/jackin/src/app.rs:512`, only `Enter` and `Esc` skip), so the
wait for `Current directory` covers the rest of the animation: `INTRO_END` is
`WARP_START + 95` ticks of 33 ms, 10.1 s in total, and the wait starts after the
first phrase. The shared deadline is 10 s, so the test had about 1 s of margin
and failed in the unmodified tree too (load 64 to 112).

### Fix

- `src/runtime.rs`: `draw_frame` queues `CSI ? 2026 h`, runs `terminal.draw`, and
  ends with `CSI ? 2026 l` even when the draw fails. All four draw calls use it.
  `restore_terminal` ends a synchronized update first, so a panic inside a draw
  cannot leave the terminal buffering. A terminal that does not know the mode
  ignores it. No cell, colour or cursor position changes: the 15 approved
  baselines replay unchanged.
- `tests/functional/showcase.rs`: the keyboard activation test waits for
  `Cancel ✓` and asserts that `Preview ✓` is gone. The assertion is stricter
  than before, and the frozen baseline already showed `Cancel ✓`.
- `tests/functional/support.rs`, `apps/jackin/tests/functional/jackin.rs`:
  `wait_text_after_animation` gives one wait the shared deadline plus the length of the animation
  it covers. The first-use test passes `rain::INTRO_END * rain::TICK_MS`. A
  missing text still fails, 10 s after the animation should have ended.

After the fix the wire carries `\x1b[?2026h ... \x1b[?2026l` around every frame,
and the same frames are still split into 1024-byte reads, so the emulator holds
each frame until its end.

### Evidence

Probe (above): 12 torn snapshots in 185 runs before, 0 in 190 after.

Full-run stress with 10 CPU burners (shell loops started and killed by PID
between runs), `cargo nextest run --locked -j 8` on the `functional` target,
33 tests in `jackin-console`, 96 in `junie-tui`. Other agents loaded the machine
as well, so every run records the load average at its start and end. The two
trees ran alternately, run by run, to see the same load. Scripts and logs are in
the session scratchpad.

| Set | Tree | `jackin-console` | `junie-tui` |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| A, unpaired, load 28 to 120 | before | 0 of 10 | 2 of 10 |
| B, paired, load 52 to 138 | before | 1 of 10 | 0 of 10 |
| B | after, without the intro budget | 1 of 10 | 0 of 10 |
| C, paired, load 54 to 132, final tree | before | 0 of 10 | 1 of 10 |
| C | after | 0 of 10 | 0 of 10 |

Failures, with the load average (1 minute, at the end of the run):

- A, `junie-tui` run 4, load 120.5: `showcase_wheel_scrolls_pointed_grid_without_moving_sidebar`, torn sidebar (above).
- A, `junie-tui` run 8, load 64.5: `showcase_keyboard_activation_after_click_focus`, stale wait.
- B, `jackin-console` run 4 before, load 112.3, and run 5 after (without the
  intro budget), load 100.8: `jackin_first_use_empty_manager_and_intro`, the
  10 s budget.
- C, `junie-tui` run 2 before, load 60.8: four `tablepro` tests timed out at the
  10 s deadline during a load spike (`visible Filter connections`, `visible Query 1`).
  Not diagnosed further; it is CPU starvation, not a torn frame.

Plain full runs at 33 tests do not hit the project-row window often (0 of 10
before), so they do not separate the trees well. The probe and the recorded
failures carry the proof. With the first fix applied but before the budget fix,
a run at load 100 to 190 (not paired, so not in the table) failed
`jackin_first_use_empty_manager_and_intro` in 7 of 10 and also
`tablepro_history` and `tablepro_write_gate` once each with a one-cell and a
`cursor.col` mismatch of a settled screen. The unmodified tree fails the first-use test
the same way at that load (3 of 3 alone at load 160 to 185).

### Not fixed: `tablepro_write_gate` and the hidden cursor

`tablepro_write_confirmation_cancels_retries_and_executes_only_when_armed` fails
its frozen check with `changed_fields: ["cursor.col"]`: candidate column 85, row
49; baseline column 159, row 49. The test alone, with 10 burners, failed 8 of 30
runs on the unmodified tree and 7 of 30 on the fixed tree, so the fix above
does not change it. It failed in 1 of the 3 full gate runs of this work (the other
two passed 339 of 339), and the B3b gate recorded the same mismatch.

What the raw output shows. ratatui does not park a hidden cursor, so the
cursor of the snapshot is the end of the last cell the last diff wrote. In green
runs the last frame is the status flash clearing: `CSI 50;126 H` plus 34 spaces,
1.3 to 2.9 s after the frame with `No open tabs`, so the cursor ends at column 159.
In red runs no frame follows the `No open tabs` frame, whose last write is the
footer hints ending at column 85. The flash was already gone when that frame was
drawn, because the status lifetime appears to run on the wall clock (inferred from the
timing, not read to the end in the status code) and the test, slowed by load,
reached the end after it expired. The frozen file therefore records the
order "flash clears last". I could not turn it red or green by one edit of the
code, and a test-side wait cannot restore the order.

Two fixes exist and neither is made here. Parking the hidden cursor at a fixed
cell in `draw_frame` makes the order irrelevant, but it moves `cursor.col` in
the approved baselines, which D27 forbids. Holding the status clock in this
test with the test clock (`JUNIE_TUI_TEST_CLOCK`, `advance_clock`) keeps the
baseline, but it needs a decision on how the status expiry reads that clock.
The first-use test and the settled-screen mismatches at load above 150 (one
changed cell in `tablepro_history`, seen once) are the same family: a
wall-clock expiry ahead of a slow test.

### Quarantined

`tablepro_write_confirmation_cancels_retries_and_executes_only_when_armed` is
marked `#[ignore]` with this section as its tracking reference.

- It fails its frozen check with `changed_fields: ["cursor.col"]` (candidate 85,
  baseline 159), 8 of 30 runs under 10 burners.
- Cause: the status flash lifetime runs on wall time, so the last position of
  the hidden cursor depends on timing.
- Fix needed: the status expiry reads the test clock, or the hidden cursor is
  parked without moving approved baselines. Remove the `#[ignore]` with that fix.
- It is independent of the synchronized-output fix.
- Resolved: the `#[ignore]` is removed in "PTY load flakes" (status clock stepped, 0 of 30 under 24 burners).

### Test plan for test-reviewer

No test was written. The fix changes behaviour, so AGENTS.md asks for a
regression test that fails on the old code. Proposed, for review:

1. A functional test in `tests/functional` that spawns `showcase`, records the
   raw output with `Operation::StartRecording` (cast), presses one key that
   repaints, and asserts that the recorded output after the key is a sequence of
   `CSI ? 2026 h` ... `CSI ? 2026 l` pairs with no cell write outside a pair.
   It is red on HEAD `8978130` (no bracket) and green after the fix. A break:
   remove the `queue!` of `BeginSynchronizedUpdate` in `draw_frame`.
2. A stress variant for the project-row window: the probe above as a test that
   opens the Project tab 100 times and asserts that no snapshot shows
   `Destination` without `~/src/payments-platform`. Red 8 times in 150 on HEAD,
   0 in 150 after.
3. For the stale wait, red/green by the reviewer's choice: delay `Tab` and
   `Enter` by one frame in a break and see the old wait pass and the new wait
   still see `Cancel ✓`.

Suggestions, not done: turn the 15 asserting predicates into total ones (a
`find_all` based check that returns false), and replace the `||` predicates that
a pre-action frame already satisfies (`showcase.rs` lines 443, 747, 939 and the
`tablepro.rs` ones) with waits for the post-action state.

### B8-0 gate: the two baseline tests

Gate (design 6.2, test plan T41): `showcase_page_terminalpane` and `showcase_terminal_replay_mid_sequence` pass, and `git status --porcelain -- tests/functional/baselines apps/jackin/tests/functional/baselines` prints nothing.

#### Result on the committed tree: `showcase_terminal_replay_mid_sequence` is red

Command: `cargo nextest run --locked -j 4 -p junie-tui --profile functional -E 'test(showcase_page_terminalpane) | test(showcase_terminal_replay_mid_sequence)'`

```text
PASS showcase_page_terminalpane
FAIL showcase_terminal_replay_mid_sequence
visual mismatch for functional/showcase_terminal_replay: ... baseline_sha256: "669c530993f4301cf44a3653a90865b4cdf2325b9aefeb8c21c8a1d7c89323f2" ... changed_fields: ["cursor.col", "cursor.row"], changed_cells: [], changed_cell_total: 0, pixel_changed: 0
Summary 2 tests run: 1 passed, 1 failed, 94 skipped   (exit 100)
```

The sample differs from the baseline in two fields only: the recorded cursor, from column 102 row 12 to column 91 row 13. No cell and no pixel differs. The baseline file is untouched (`git status --porcelain -- tests/functional/baselines apps/jackin/tests/functional/baselines` prints nothing). It was not accepted.

Cause, shown by the saved evidence frames of the failing run: termpane keeps the visible rows when the program clears the screen with `ESC[2J` (preserve-on-clear, `DamageGrid` private `preserve_visible_rows_to_scrollback`; `vt100` drops them). The `midstream` fixture ends with `ESC[2J ESC[H ready $ `, so after the third Step the playground side panel reads `scrollback  3 rows · viewing 0 up` where `vt100` read `0 rows`. The next click on Replay builds a fresh state, which repaints that line to `0 rows`. Rows 12 and 13 of the screen (0-based) are the panel lines `cursor  row 2, col 1` and `scrollback`. The recorded cursor follows the last cell that the frame diff painted: row 12 in the baseline, where the `cursor` line is the last line to change, and row 13 in the candidate, where the `scrollback` line changes too. The forced-zero run below confirms this reading. The saved frames of the termpane run read `scrollback  0 rows · viewing 0 up` in checkpoints 000 to 008 and 012 to 014, and `3 rows` in checkpoints 009 to 011 (after the third Step, before the second Replay). The differential run below shows `vt100` at 0 rows after the same clear.

Proof that this is the only difference. A temporary edit (reverted, not committed) made `TerminalState::scrollback_len` return `0 * self.grid.scrollback_len()`:

```text
PASS showcase_page_terminalpane
PASS showcase_terminal_replay_mid_sequence
Summary 2 tests run: 2 passed, 94 skipped   (exit 0)
```

No adapter change keeps both the baseline bytes and the truth of `scrollback_len`, because termpane has no switch for preserve-on-clear (`termpane-0.1.0` `src/grid.rs`, no public knob). The decision is open: see the report to the orchestrator.

#### Differential run against vt100 (design 6.2, recorded once, not merged)

A temporary integration test with `vt100 0.18.1` as a dev-dependency (reverted) fed the four showcase fixtures (`session`, `fullscreen`, `split`, `midstream`) to both emulators, at 12x40, 6x36, 6x20 and 24x80, from each of the four replay starts, with and without a trailing `ESC[2J ESC[H$ ` redraw chunk, and compared the whole grid after every chunk: 640 comparison steps.

```text
cell differences (contents, wide continuation, fg, bg, bold, dim, italic, underline, inverse): 0
modes (application cursor, bracketed paste, alternate screen, hide cursor): 0 differences
scrollback_len: 120 differences, every one after an ESC[2J (termpane preserves the cleared rows, vt100 does not)
cursor position: 4 differences, split fixture at 6x20, vt100 reports column 20 (pending wrap), the adapter clamps to 19
```

The cursor clamp is the design's change (6.2): the old adapter hid the cursor in that state, the new one draws it on the last column. No baseline reaches that state.

### B8-0 completion

Decision (operator, 2026-11-04): `termpane` is a git dependency pinned to `0707acca3cbf3333d4c1b0e6d88b7b69169233dc` (termpane `main`, with `EraseDisplayPolicy` from PR #35, merged as f1c2dfde) until 0.1.1 is published on crates.io. `TerminalState::with_scrollback` calls `grid.set_erase_display_policy(EraseDisplayPolicy::Discard)`; `reset()` goes through `with_scrollback`. This removes the open decision in "Result on the committed tree" above: `ESC[2J` no longer moves the visible rows into history, so the replay baseline keeps `scrollback  0 rows`.

## B5b: Backend::file_source, Instance data, the daemon label (BX21, G13 d and e, F37)

Plan: design v7.1 section 5.1 (rows "File browser" and "Instance data") and section 7 (B5b), test plan v7.1 T40 with its review verdict (GO). Tests: `apps/jackin/tests/sources.rs`, feature `demo`, eight tests. T40 lists five names; the two GitHub tests each have a manager half and a capsule half, and the capsule halves are separate tests (`capsule_github_comes_from_the_instance`, `capsule_name_suffix_no_longer_matches`) so that a red manager half does not hide the capsule half. A third test, `wizard_working_dirs_come_from_the_source`, covers the wizard half of the T40 positive case.

Decisions made while building:

- `Backend::file_source(&World) -> Box<dyn FileSource>` is a provided method. Its default builds a `MemorySource` from `World.fs` with the mapping the file browser used (`git · {branch}` for a repository, `meta` otherwise) and `World.home`. `SimBackend` keeps the default, so the demo lists the same rows. `file_browser()` in `screens/modals.rs` and `workdir_candidates` in `screens/prelude.rs` read only `Backend::file_source`. A list error in the wizard working-dir step gives no sub-folder rows (the browser step already shows the error inline).
- `Instance.github: Option<RepoRef>`; `RepoRef { full_name, url }` is a domain type. `GithubRepo` stays the browser's record. The demo fixtures and the demo cockpit (until B7a rebuilds it) set `github` from the one suffix match that used to sit in the screens. The manager `o` key on a workspace reads the first instance of that workspace that has a repository (`World::workspace_repo`). The capsule reads `Instance.github`.
- `World::workspace_label(&Instance)` is the daemon label and the `Image` row name: the workspace name from `Instance.workspace`, or the last cwd segment for an instance without a workspace (what the launch used before). `workdir` is no longer parsed.
- `tests/vocabulary.rs::the_github_dialog_copies_nothing_without_a_matching_repository` made the repository unrelated by renaming `World.github[0]`. That is the name matching this step removes, so the test now clears `Instance.github`. Its assertions are unchanged.
- Not moved: `screens/editor.rs` (working-dir picker, `w.fs` at line 1687) still reads `World.fs`; it formats the entry meta without the `git ·` prefix, so the default source cannot serve it byte for byte. It belongs to the B6 editor rewrite.

Red: the commit head `7424497` plus the type scaffold only (the `RepoRef` type, `Instance.github` set to `None` in `fixtures` and the cockpit, and the provided `Backend::file_source`, none of it read by a screen). Without the scaffold the tests do not compile, and a compile error is not a disagreement. Command: `cargo nextest run --locked -j 4 -p jackin-console --all-features --test sources --no-fail-fast`, exit 100, `8 tests run: 0 passed, 8 failed`.

```text
daemon_label_from_workspace            left: "/sandbox/project"   right: "payments-platform"
github_comes_from_the_instance         left: Some("Opened https://github.com/chainargos/payments-platform on the host")
                                       right: Some("Opened https://github.com/acme/elsewhere on the host")
name_suffix_no_longer_matches          left: Some("Opened https://github.com/chainargos/payments-platform on the host")
                                       right: Some("No GitHub source for payments-platform")
capsule_github_comes_from_the_instance left: Some("https://github.com/chainargos/payments-platform/tree/main")
                                       right: Some("https://github.com/acme/elsewhere/tree/main")
capsule_name_suffix_no_longer_matches  left: Some(("Opened the pull request on the host (gated host action)", Secondary))
                                       right: Some(("No GitHub URL to open for this instance", Error))
```

`file_browser_uses_the_backend_source`, `source_error_is_inline` and `wizard_working_dirs_come_from_the_source` fail on `assert!(shown.contains(..))` and print the screen: the New workspace browser at `/home/u/code` (and at `/denied`) shows only the `..` row, because the screen still reads the empty `World.fs`; the wizard step 4 lists only `/home/u/code` (destination), without `/home/u/code/alpha/inner`.

Green, after the screens read the new data:
