## BG: the gaps the jackin console found (J1, J1b)

Plan: the J1 and J1b reviews (`P9.3-J1-review`, findings 3 and 5, the decision rows of 2026-11-08T21:00 and 22:00) and the J1b doctor items 11 to 16. The rule behind every row: jackin writes no screen text, pixel or screen code, so every sentence comes from `Vocabulary`, `Fixed` or surfaces data, and every live fact arrives through `Backend`, `Snapshot` and the reply types. No approved baseline moved (D27) and the demo is byte-identical: the simulator fills every new field, and `git status --porcelain` over both baseline directories is empty after the full run.

Tests: `apps/jackin/tests/gaps.rs` (feature `demo`, 19 tests: 18 for the gaps and one guard), and `a_failed_event_without_a_record_adds_no_instance` in `tests/launch_feed.rs`. One guard, `a_gateway_that_answered_keeps_a_report_of_skips_out_of_failing`, passes before and after: a report with a gateway and only skipped rows stays healthy, so gap 16 does not turn every skip into a failure.

### Gap to API table, for the jackin side

| # | Gap | Baseline API | What jackin does now |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | `OpError` has no "not available" | `OpError::NotAvailable` (no fields). The picker shows `1Password is not available` and `Install the 1Password CLI and sign in, then press r to retry`; `message()` has the row text for a validation. Not retryable (`retryable()` stays `Locked` only). | Answers `Msg::OpLoaded` with `Err(OpError::NotAvailable)` when `op` cannot run. |
| 2 | `FailedLaunch.instance` not optional | `FailedLaunch.instance: Option<Instance>`. `None` adds no roster row and the cockpit acknowledges with no instance. | Sends `None` and stops building a `failed setup` record. |
| 3 | `spawn_pane` cannot fail or say pending; replies of `changes`, `probe_folder`, `validate` | **Moved to B8b** (pane lifecycle: `PaneLink`, pending, attaching, failed) by the coordinator's scope change; BG changes nothing in `PaneBody`, `Backend::spawn_pane` or the capsule. The reply check stays in BG and needs no code: the replies of `CheckSource`, `ProbeFolder` and `ReadChanges` are `Result<_, String>` and `LoadOp` is `Result<_, OpError>`, so they carry a failure already (`pending.rs`: `a_failed_check_shows_the_reply_error_in_the_form`, `a_folder_probe_that_fails_keeps_the_form_open`, `a_failed_changes_read_shows_the_reply_error`). `Unbuilt::Pane` ("A terminal pane is not available in this build yet") stays one of the 20 `Unbuilt` sentences for B8b to use. | Nothing in BG. |
| 4 | `TrustRow.kind` is `&'static str` | `TrustRow.kind: TrustKind { Git, Path }`, `TrustKind::label()` (`git`, `path`). | Passes the enum; `KIND_GIT` and `KIND_PATH` go. |
| 5 | `RoleSource::Git.branch` required | `branch: Option<String>`; `RoleSource::label()` is `url @ branch` or `url`. | Passes `rev.map(..)` with no `unwrap_or_default`. |
| 6 | About labels and values, `DiscoveryError::label` | `LiveFacts.gateway: Option<String>`; `Surfaces::openshell` builds `about` from `Vocabulary.about_product`, `about_gateway`, `about_config` and the facts (the gateway row only when there is a gateway). `Surfaces::about(..)` is removed. `DiscoveryError::label()` is removed for `Vocabulary::discovery(DiscoveryError)` and the field `discovery_unreadable` (`sandbox list unreadable` live, `instance index unreadable` in the demo). | Drops `AboutLabel` and the `.about(..)` call, passes the gateway name in `LiveFacts`. |
| 7 | Error dialog `Next` row is fixed | `SaveFailure { error: String, next: Option<String> }` is the `Err` of `Msg::WorkspaceSaved` and `Msg::GlobalSaved`. The dialog shows `Next` only when `next` is `Some`. The simulator fills it from `Vocabulary.save_error_next`. | Builds `SaveFailure` from its error type, with the advice the error carries or `None`. |
| 8 | `Provider` lacks three | `Provider::Antigravity`, `Hermes`, `Muse` (the other 11 of jackin-core's 14 were there): `label`, `short`, `plain_key_label`, `folder_label`, no usage surface, no agent. | `provider()` is total; `unmapped_accounts` and the `AccountNotShown` note go. |
| 9 | Refresh drops gateway health | `Snapshot.daemon_health: DaemonHealth`; `World::apply` replaces `World.daemon_health`, so a recovery clears `Stale` and a failed listing on a connected gateway sets it. `Msg::Refreshed { Err }` still sets `Stale`. | Fills `daemon_health` from the observation (`Stale` when any gateway failed to list). |
| 10a | 20 `Unbuilt` sentences | `Unbuilt` (the 20 variants) and `Vocabulary::unbuilt(Unbuilt) -> String`, from the field `not_built` (`{what} is not available in this build yet`); the subjects follow `container_noun`, so the sandbox ones say `sandbox`. | Replies `Err(vocabulary.unbuilt(Unbuilt::X))`; its own `Unbuilt` and `Display` go. |
| 10b | observe `Note` sentences | `Notice::{GatewayUnreachable { gateway, reason }, UnreadableLabels { count }}`, `Vocabulary::notice(&Notice)` and `notices(&[Notice])` (joined with ` · `), from `gateway_unreachable` and `labels_unreadable`. `AccountNotShown` needs none (gap 8). The reason of a timeout (`no answer within N s`) stays an error text of jackin. | Builds `Vec<Notice>` and calls `Vocabulary::openshell().notices(..)` where it has no world. |
| 10c | `tls port N` | `EngineState::Configured { endpoint: EngineEndpoint }`, `EngineEndpoint::{Address(String), TlsPort(NonZeroU16)}`, `Vocabulary::engine_endpoint`, field `engine_tls_port`. The demo keeps `Address("host.openshell.internal:18476")`. | Passes `TlsPort(port)`. |
| 10d | `jk-` prefix | `domain::instance::ID_PREFIX` and `instance_id(suffix)`; the screens trim with the same constant. | Calls `instance_id(suffix)`. |
| 11 | `DoctorFinished` cannot say the checks could not run | `Msg::DoctorFinished(Result<DoctorReport, DoctorFailure>)`, `DoctorFailure::{TimedOut, Failed(String)}`, `DoctorState::Failed { previous, failure, at_secs }`, `Vocabulary::doctor_failure` from `doctor_failed` and `doctor_timed_out`. The earlier report stays on screen; with none, the empty state carries the words. The chrome is `Failing`. | Sends `Err(..)` where it sent a fabricated failing report. |
| 12 | `DoctorCheck.expected` has no source | It was `Option<String>` already. The detail now draws the `Expected` row only when it is `Some`, as the demo does (every demo check has one); `None` used to draw `—`. | Sends `None`. |
| 13 | No doctor timer | `DoctorSurface.rerun_secs: Option<i64>` (`None` in the demo). `World::tick` calls `World::start_doctor` (the same transition the `r` key uses, then `Effect::RunDoctor`) when `DoctorSurface::due`: never run, or that many seconds after the last report or failure; never while a run is out. Reason for the baseline: `Backend::due` returns `Msg`s only, so a timer there could not set `Running` (the spinner, `previous`, the single-flight rule), and the baseline already owns the 5 s refresh timer. | Sets `rerun_secs` and answers `Effect::RunDoctor`; no timer of its own. |
| 14 | A refresh overwrites the doctor | `World::apply` keeps the existing `DoctorSurface.state` (`Done`, `Running`, `Failed`) and takes the rest of `surfaces` from the snapshot; a snapshot that brings the doctor in first sets its state, and one without a doctor removes it. Only `Msg::DoctorFinished` and `World::start_doctor` change the state. | Needs no version. |
| 15 | `health_view` ignores `daemon_health` once a report exists | Order: a failed run, a failing check or no gateway is `Failing`; a stale report or `daemon_health == Stale` is `Stale` (age from `last_refresh_secs` for the listing); else `Healthy`. | Sets `daemon_health` in the snapshot (gap 9). |
| 16 | No usable gateway gives a healthy chrome | `DoctorReport::no_gateway()` (`gateway: None`) makes the chrome `Failing`; the Doctor page head reads `degraded · no usable gateway` (`doctor_no_gateway`) instead of `all checks pass`. | Sends `gateway: None` when the config names none. |

The Vocabulary gains 11 fields (`not_built`, `gateway_unreachable`, `labels_unreadable`, `about_product`, `about_gateway`, `about_config`, `discovery_unreadable`, `engine_tls_port`, `doctor_failed`, `doctor_timed_out`, `doctor_no_gateway`). `Vocabulary::demo()` fills each with a word that differs from `openshell()`, so `the_openshell_vocabulary_renames_the_demo_words` and `the_openshell_words_avoid_the_false_strings` hold; only `discovery_unreadable` keeps the exact demo text (`instance index unreadable`), because the demo shows it. Compile breaks for jackin: `LiveFacts.gateway`, `Snapshot.daemon_health`, `DoctorSurface.rerun_secs`, `FailedLaunch.instance`, `TrustRow.kind`, `RoleSource::Git.branch`, `EngineState::Configured.endpoint`, `Msg::WorkspaceSaved` and `GlobalSaved`, `Msg::DoctorFinished`, `Surfaces::about`, `DiscoveryError::label`.

### Red

The red tree is the finished code with a type-only scaffold: every new type, field and function exists and the simulator fills it, but no screen reads the new data and each new function is a stub (an empty string, the old text, the old branch). It is the committed tree plus `unwire.py`, a script of 23 one-edit reverts (one per gap, in this order: the picker arm; the cockpit builds a `failed setup` row when `instance` is `None`; the trust screen ignores `kind`; `label()` prints `url @ `; `about` stays empty and `discovery` returns the old label; the dialogs print `save_error_next`; the three provider labels are empty; `apply` skips `daemon_health`; `unbuilt`, `notices`, `engine_endpoint` and `instance_id` are stubs; `finish` ignores an `Err`; `Expected` always draws; no timer; `apply` overwrites the doctor; the listing is ignored in `health_view`; `no_gateway()` is `false`). The script is not committed. Command: `cargo nextest run --locked -j 4 -p jackin-console --all-features --test gaps --test launch_feed --test pane_body --no-fail-fast`, exit 100, `55 tests run: 36 passed, 19 failed`. The 19 are the 18 gap tests of `gaps.rs` and the one in `launch_feed.rs`; the 36 passes are the guard and the 35 older tests of the three files.

```text
a_missing_one_password_says_it_is_not_available        missing `1Password is not available` (the picker printed the raw message)
a_failed_event_without_a_record_adds_no_instance       left: 10  right: 9   (the cockpit added a failed setup row)
a_trust_row_opens_by_its_kind_and_shows_the_kind       missing `~/src/github.com/me/roles path` (the column said git)
a_git_role_without_a_branch_shows_its_url_alone        left: "github.com/acme/no-branch @ "  right: "github.com/acme/no-branch"
the_live_about_rows_use_the_vocabulary_labels          left: []  right: [("Product", "1.4.0"), ("Gateway", "local-gateway"), ("Config", ...)]
the_discovery_error_reads_from_the_vocabulary          missing `! sandbox list unreadable` (the strip said `! instance index unreadable`)
the_save_error_next_row_is_the_errors_own              missing `Next free some disk space, then Save again` (the dialog said `check disk space and permissions`)
the_providers_jackin_knows_are_all_here                left: ""  right: "Antigravity"
a_snapshot_replaces_the_gateway_health                 left: Healthy  right: Stale
a_failed_listing_shows_even_when_a_doctor_report_exists left: Healthy  right: Stale
no_usable_gateway_is_a_failing_chrome                  left: Healthy  right: Failing   (all skipped)
a_refresh_leaves_the_doctor_state_alone                left: Done(.. "Older" ..)  right: Done(.. "Newer" ..)
a_doctor_that_could_not_run_says_why                   missing `no answer in time` (the page still showed `running checks`)
the_expected_value_is_drawn_only_when_the_check_has_one unexpected `Expected` in the detail of a check with none
the_world_reruns_the_doctor_when_its_timer_runs_out    100 s at one run per 30 s and 1 s of checking is 3 runs, got 0

the_notices_are_the_vocabularys                        left: ""  right: "Could not list sandboxes on local: no answer within 5 s"

```

An earlier red run had four `launch_feed` guards (`the_sim_launch_ends_as_it_did`, `the_sim_abort_records_a_failed_setup`, `the_sim_cancel_records_a_failed_setup`, `the_sim_failure_records_a_failed_setup`) fail because the stub of `instance_id` also drove the simulator's ids. The script now puts the old `format!("jk-{:04x}", ..)` back in the simulator, and they pass in the red tree, as they did on `29b9a6c`. Another run died with `attempt to subtract with overflow` in the timer test: that was a bad revert (`due(i64::MIN)`), not a disagreement, and the revert is now `due(..) && false`.

## Batch fix round (B5, B7b, B8-1)

Reviews: `P9.3-B5-B7b-B81-review` (MEDIUM 1, LOW 2), `P9.3-B5-B7b-B81-ux` (F1 to F4) and `P9.3-B5-B7b-B81-redgreen` (the `PlainKey` gap). Base `29b9a6c`. Under D27 no approved baseline moves except the exact files the UX findings re-take. Every red below is a run on the unfixed code of `29b9a6c` with the new test in place (or, where the test passes on the code and guards a behavior the code already has, a named break of that code); the fix then turned it green.

### MEDIUM 1: a check reply is the form's only while the form holds what the request was made from

A pending check was dropped only by `form_changed`, which the form raises when a field commits, so typing into a text field left the check alive and a stale reply saved or showed the pre-edit value. `Check` now keeps `request`, the values the request was built from. Before a `SourceChecked` or `FolderProbed` reply is delivered, the app asks each open form's screen (`Screen::reply_arrives`) and the accounts screen drops the check, and ends the pending rows, when the form's values differ from `request`. The reply then finds no check and is ignored. A Save or Validate that finds a check made from other values starts a new check instead of joining it. The `"plain"` action calls `cancel_check` like the two chooser buttons.

Red (`cargo nextest run --locked -j 4 -p jackin-console --all-features --test pending --no-fail-fast`, exit 100, `28 tests run: 24 passed, 4 failed`):

```text
a_probe_reply_for_a_path_the_user_has_since_edited_saves_nothing   the old path is not stored   left: 1   right: 0
a_save_whose_check_reply_finds_the_form_edited_stores_nothing      a reply for the old path starts no probe
a_check_reply_for_a_key_the_user_has_since_edited_is_not_shown     the rows describe the old key: the form shows `✓ material   reply from the backend`
entering_plain_text_drops_the_pending_check                        the pending rows are gone: the form still shows `… material   checking`
```

The first test is the review's scenario: Save a local folder, deliver the check reply, click into the folder field and type `x y z`, deliver the stale `FolderProbed`. Before the fix `SaveAccounts` went out with `~/.claude-spare`; now nothing is stored, the form stays open and shows the typed text. Green: `28 tests run: 28 passed`.

Two tests pass on the fixed code and on the code before the round, because `choose:op` and `choose:browse` already call `cancel_check`; they are the missing guard of the review (`b5_3`, `b5_4`). Each was shown red by removing the call, and green again after the revert:

```text
choosing_a_reference_drops_the_pending_check   (no cancel_check in choose:op)       the reply to a dropped check says nothing: Validated · Save stores the reference, never the secret
browsing_for_a_folder_drops_the_pending_check  (no cancel_check in choose:browse)   the chooser buttons drop the pending check
```

### UX F1: the pending reference line is vocabulary

`only the reference is saved · the value is resolved at launch` is false for jackin (D28: the host reads the reference for usage only). The line is `Vocabulary::op_reference_note`, in its own block of `domain/vocabulary.rs` and `fixtures/vocabulary.rs` (after `agent_no_credentials`) to keep the merge with the other `Vocabulary` work small. Demo: the old text, so `jackin_account_form_pending` does not move. OpenShell: `only the reference is saved · read here for usage only` (54 columns; the first wording, `jackin reads it on this machine for usage`, was cut by the 70-wide form). `the_openshell_words_avoid_the_false_strings` and `the_openshell_vocabulary_renames_the_demo_words` cover the field through `fields()`.

Red (`--test pending`, `the_reference_note_follows_the_vocabulary`, the openshell world after a reference is chosen):

```text
panicked at apps/jackin/tests/pending.rs:677:5   (the form row reads `only the reference is saved · the value is resolved at launch`)
```

Green. New baseline `jackin_fixed_account_form_pending` (journey `jackin_fixed_account_form_says_what_jackin_does_with_a_reference`, scenario `openshell-fixed`, same steps as `jackin_account_form_pending`): the `(usage only)` radio, the `Usage only · launching Codex needs a local login` note, the OpenShell reference line and the three `checking` rows. Accepted with `--expect-baseline missing`. The three other sites the review named (`app.rs` picker, `config.rs` env rows) are not changed.

### UX F2: the picker reason fits

`AgentBlocked::openshell().text` is `usage only · launches Codex only` (32 columns; it was 39 in a 38-column cell and lost `ly`). Tests changed: `surfaces_openshell_sets_every_field`, the `OTHER_AGENT` constant and the picker row in `tests/account_data.rs`, and the journey `jackin_fixed_launch_picker_disables_every_agent_jackin_cannot_launch`.

Red (the tests changed, the text not): `surfaces_openshell_sets_every_field` `left: "usage only · jackin launches Codex only"  right: "usage only · launches Codex only"`; the journey `Claude Code is a disabled row with its reason`. Green after the text change.

### UX F3: the refusal and the env prompt sit over the Codex cockpit

`SimBackend::guarded()` runs the OpenShell plan (`openshell: true`: seven stages), and `surfaces::launch_checks` sets `agent_blocked`, so Codex is the first launchable agent of the picker in `launch-checks`. The refusal and the questions now sit over `Codex · OpenAI · account Codex …` and `0/7 stages`: `Image build`, `OpenShell provider`, `Sandbox`, `Credentials`, `Upload`, `Hooks`, `Codex`.

Red (`--test launch_checks`, `the_checks_sit_over_the_codex_cockpit_of_seven_stages`, both the refusal and the questions): `customer-portal › writer:` the screen reads `Claude Code · Anthropic / Claude · account Claude · Personal` and `0/11 stages`, and `Codex · OpenAI` is missing. Green.

Deviation: the questions open on `infra-control-plane`, whose workspace names the Codex account `Experiments`, so that cockpit reads `account Codex · Experiments (Workspace choice)`, not `Primary (provider default)` as in `jackin_launch_openshell`.

### UX F4: an entry with no default is required

`open_questions` sets `.required(q.default.is_none())` and `required_message("Enter a value for {NAME}.")`. A field with a default stays `optional`. `the_sim_asks_what_the_role_manifest_asks` clicked `Start launch` with `SENTINEL_NOTE` empty; it now types a value first (the entry has no default). The journey `jackin_launch_asks_for_the_values_a_role_needs` does the same after it checks that an empty start is refused.

Red (`--test launch_feed`, `an_entry_without_a_default_cannot_start_empty`): `an entry with no default is marked required:` the `Note for the run` row reads `optional`. Green: the row shows `*`, an empty `Start launch` shows `Enter a value for SECOND_VALUE.` with the form open and no answer sent, and a typed value is sent in the feed's order.

### Red/green gap: `PlainKey` Debug

The `Debug` impl already prints `PlainKey(…)`, so the test passes without a code change. `the_typed_key_never_reaches_a_debug_print` (`--test pending`) sends a typed key through Validate and checks `{:?}` and `{:#?}` of the effects the backend saw and of the `SourceChecked` reply. Break: `write!(f, "PlainKey({:?})", self.0)` in `backend.rs`:

```text
the_typed_key_never_reaches_a_debug_print   the effect prints the key: [CheckSource { ticket: 1, provider: Anthropic, source: PlainApiKey { fingerprint: "a605d51c", tail: "1234" }, key: Some(PlainKey("sk-ant-valid-abcdef1234")) }]
```

Reverted: green.

### LOW 2: a drag after the area collapsed

`on_drag` of `TerminalViewport` and `TextViewport` ends the press and returns `Ignored` when the area (the text rectangle for `TextViewport`) is empty. A press with a non-empty area, then `set_area(Rect::new(3, 2, 0, 0))`, then `on_drag((2, 2))` panicked in `clamp`.

Red (`--test terminal_viewport --test viewport_ops -E 'test(/collapsed/)'`, exit 100):

```text
a_drag_after_the_area_collapsed_is_ignored                       panicked at src/terminal/viewport.rs:514:18: min > max. min = 3, max = 2
a_text_viewport_drag_after_the_area_collapsed_is_ignored         panicked at src/widgets/viewport.rs:1395:19: min > max. min = 3, max = 2
```

Green. Each test also restores the area and drags again: the press ended with the collapse, so nothing is selected.

### Re-accepted and new baselines

Each was accepted with `tuiscotti accept --candidate <sample> --candidate-sha256 <sha> --expect-baseline <sha256 of the old file, or missing> --baselines apps/jackin/tests/functional/baselines`, after a cell diff of the candidate against the committed file. The cells of the two F3 candidates were identical in four runs. No other baseline moved.

| `jackin_fixed_launch_picker` | 16, 18, 19, 20, 21 (120 cells, text and fg): `usage only · jackin launches Codex on…` becomes `usage only · launches Codex only` | F2 |
| `jackin_role_not_allowed` | 0 (`0/11` becomes `0/7 stages`), 5 (`Codex · OpenAI · account Codex · Primary (provider default)`), 8 to 14 (the seven Codex stages). Dialog rows 15 to 24 keep their text | F3 |
| `jackin_launch_env_prompt` | 0, 5 (`Codex · OpenAI · account Codex · Experiments (Workspace choice)`), 6 (`stage 1 of 7 · Image build`), 8 to 12 (the stages), 18 (`Note for the run  optional` becomes `Note for the run *`) | F3, F4 |

Beyond the rows the UX review named, in the two F3 files: rows 16 and 17 differ only in the `fg` of blank cells inside the dialog, and row 39 differs only in the `bold` flag of footer cells (`Clo[s][e]` is bold in the new `jackin_role_not_allowed`, blank cells after `Close` were bold in the old one). No text differs there. They come from the frame history of the capture, are the same in four runs, and are flagged for the `ux-specialist` review.

## BG2: the gaps the jackin persistence step found (J2)

Plan: the J2 baseline gaps (`rewrite/reconciliation/P9.3.md`, "Baseline gaps", items 1, 3, 5, 6, 7, 8 and 10) and the decision row of 2026-11-09T00:30. Base `516d522`. The rule is the one of BG: jackin writes no screen text and no id, every sentence comes from `Vocabulary` or `Fixed`, and every live fact arrives through `Backend`, `Snapshot` and the reply types. No approved baseline moved (D27) and the demo is byte-identical: the simulator and the fixtures fill every new field, and `git status --porcelain` over both baseline directories is empty after the full run.

Tests: `apps/jackin/tests/gaps_bg2.rs` (feature `demo`, 19 tests: 13 for the gaps and 6 guards that pass before and after). The guards pin what must not move: `the_demo_save_status_is_unchanged`, `the_demo_wizard_keeps_git_pull_on`, `every_demo_workspace_keeps_the_repo_its_instances_gave` (all 16 worlds), `no_demo_world_offers_several_accounts_without_a_choice` (all 16 worlds, every workspace, the first eight roles and no role, every agent), `the_demo_purge_refusal_is_the_reason_alone` and `a_preselected_account_launches_without_a_second_pick`.

Facts checked before building:

- Gap 5 and the demo. A probe (not committed) walked the 16 worlds, every workspace and the first eight roles for each agent offer and looked for two or more ready accounts whose choice was not made by a rule (`account_for` returned `None` or the `Enabled in … › Accounts` fallback): 0 hits in 3258 offers. No demo data needed changing. The case sits in `resolve_account`, not only in `offer_for`: the last step of `resolve_account` takes the first account the workspace enables as `Workspace` level when no rule chose one, so `ResolvedAccount.explicit` (false for that fallback) is the new fact `offer_for` reads.
- Gap 6. `RoleFinding.key` was already `Option<String>` at `516d522` and the screens read it as one; only `fix` changes. The test covers both being absent.
- Gap 1 wording. The test plan T33 says the name is cut to "the first 20 characters plus `…`" and also "at most 56 characters"; both cannot hold with `ui::text::truncate`, which the design names (6 + 20 + 30 = 56 columns means 19 characters plus `…`). The code uses `truncate(name, 20)`: a 25-character name shows 19 characters and `…`, and a name up to 20 columns shows whole.

### Gap to API table, for the jackin side

| # | Gap | Baseline API | What jackin does now |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | `Fixed.save_hint` | `Fixed.save_hint: String`, `Saved {name} · set a default role in Roles` in `Fixed::openshell()`. After an editor save, when `Surfaces.fixed` is `Some`, the saved workspace has no `roles.default` and `World.roles.len() > 1`, the status is the hint with `{name}` cut by `truncate(name, 20)`; otherwise `Workspace {name} saved`. The demo has no `Fixed` and shows the old text. | Nothing; `Fixed::openshell()` carries the text. T33 reads `Fixed::openshell().save_hint` and cuts the name the same way. |
| 2 | `Workspace` has no repository | `Workspace.github: Option<RepoRef>`; `World::workspace_repo(id)` reads the workspace, never an instance. `Workspace::new` sets `None`; `Workspace` is 13 fields, `github` is Data in `workspace_fixed`. The fixtures set it from the workspace's instances, so the demo is unchanged. | Fills `github` from `Session::project_origin` (`RepoRef { full_name, url }`) in the projection. Compile break: every `Workspace { .. }` literal. |
| 3 | The wizard creates `git_pull = true` | `domain::openshell::policies(&mut Workspace)` (keep awake off, git pull off, dirty exit ask; `openshell::workspace` calls it too). The wizard calls it when `Surfaces.fixed` is `Some`; the demo keeps `Workspace::new`'s `git_pull: true`. | Nothing. |
| 4 | Account ids minted by the form | `Account::is_unsaved()` and `UNSAVED_ID` (the empty id). The form sends the new account with `UNSAVED_ID` in `Effect::SaveAccounts { registry }`; the backend gives it an id and names it in `Msg::AccountsSaved { added: Option<AccountId>, result }`; the screen selects `added` and shows the snapshot's account. The simulator mints `acct-<provider>-<name>` (and `-2`, `-3` on a clash). | In `SaveAccounts`, the registry account with an empty id is the new one: give it its name and reply `added: Some(name)`. Compile break: `Msg::AccountsSaved`. |
| 5 | `offer_for` preselects the first of several | `AgentOffer.preselected` is `Some` only when a rule chose it (`ResolvedAccount.explicit`) or exactly one account is ready. Otherwise `None`: the launch and session pickers show `N accounts · choose at start`, enabled, and picking the agent opens `Account for <agent>` (`screens::modals::account_picker`, shared with the capsule's), then `Go::Launch` or `Go::NewSession` carries the chosen account. | Nothing; a launch always arrives with the account the user chose. |
| 6 | `RoleFinding.fix` and `key` | `RoleFinding.fix: Option<String>`; the hint row is drawn only for `Some`. `key` was `Option<String>` already. | Passes `fix: None` (jackin has no fix text) and `key: None` where it has none. Compile break: `RoleFinding { fix }`. |
| 7 | Short error sentences | `Rejection::{CannotSave(SaveField), NoProject, NotAFolder { folder }, NoRoleLeft, NotInRoster { instance }, UnknownRole { role }, RoleNotLoaded}` and `Vocabulary::rejection(&Rejection) -> String`, from the fields `cannot_save` (`{field}`), `no_project`, `not_a_folder` (`{folder}`), `no_role_left`, `not_in_roster` (`{id}`), `unknown_role` (`{role}`) and `role_not_loaded`. `SaveField` has the 23 fields of jackin's `persist::Field` (`label()` is the config key or phrase: `workdir`, `keep_awake`, `enabled accounts`, `global mounts`, …). `Unbuilt::AddGitRole` (`Adding a role from a Git URL`) is the 21st unbuilt sentence. The simulator words its missing instance and role through the same methods. | Replaces `PersistError::{Unrepresentable, NoProject, Folder, NoRoles}`, `Refused::{NotInRoster, UnknownRole}`, `RoleDocError::NotLoaded` and `Unbuilt::AddGitRole` with typed values and calls `Vocabulary::openshell().rejection(..)` and `.unbuilt(..)`. |
| 8 | The purge refusal has no next step | `LifecycleWords.purge_refused_next: Option<String>`; a refused purge shows `{error} · {next}`. OpenShell: `to delete it anyway, run jackin purge --force`; demo: `None`, so the demo shows the reason alone. The console still offers only the guarded purge. | Nothing; `Msg::Purged { Err(session text) }` is enough. |

The Vocabulary gains 7 fields (`cannot_save`, `no_project`, `not_a_folder`, `no_role_left`, `not_in_roster`, `unknown_role`, `role_not_loaded`) and `LifecycleWords` one (`purge_refused_next`). `Vocabulary::demo()` fills each with a word that differs from `openshell()`, except `not_in_roster`, which keeps the simulator's old text (`{id} is not in the roster`, the same as jackin's) and joins `status.superseded` and `capsule` in the `SAME` list of `the_openshell_vocabulary_renames_the_demo_words`. Compile breaks for jackin: `Workspace.github`, `Msg::AccountsSaved.added`, `RoleFinding.fix`.

The red tree is `516d522` plus a type-only scaffold: the new fields, types and functions exist (`Fixed.save_hint`, `Workspace.github`, `Account::is_unsaved` and `UNSAVED_ID`, `Msg::AccountsSaved.added`, `ResolvedAccount.explicit`, `RoleFinding.fix` as an `Option` drawn as before, the `Rejection` and `SaveField` types with `label()` returning an empty string, `Unbuilt::AddGitRole` with an empty subject, `Vocabulary::rejection` returning an empty string, the `Vocabulary` fields, `LifecycleWords.purge_refused_next`) and the simulator and fixtures fill them, but no screen reads the new data. ### New baselines

Each was accepted with `tuiscotti accept --candidate <sample> --candidate-sha256 <sha> --expect-baseline missing --baselines apps/jackin/tests/functional/baselines` after its text was read (`python3 view.py <sample>` prints the 120x40 grid and the dim, bold and inverse flags of each row). All are 120x40, truecolor, from `--scenario capsule-terminal --motion paused --frame 40`, after the clock was advanced to the instant named.

| Baseline | What it shows |
| --- | --- |

Under it, a bordered `Codex (Primary)` pane with one inner row (a 92% split; 4 rows is the floor) says on that row ` Pane too small · zoom with Ctrl+B z`. The Codex pane has no link yet. |
| `jackin_pane_attaching` | Tab `3 attaching`, status line cleared (6 s). One pane, no border (a single pane has none): the empty state `Attaching to Codex…` with the hint `Ctrl+B x cancels`, centred. No note row. |
| `jackin_pane_viewing` | Tab `4 viewing`, 1.5 s in. Two panes. The left title reads ` Codex (Primary) (viewing) `, the grid shows the Codex stream (bold, colours, `日本語のコメントは残します`), the note row reads ` Read-only · another client controls this pane`, and the status line carries the server notice `another client controls this pane`. The hardware cursor rests after the prompt `› ` of the focused pane. |
| `jackin_pane_detached` | Tab `5 detached`, 2.5 s in. The Codex stream is dimmed, the scroll track at rest, the note row reads ` Detached from Codex · press Enter to reattach` and the status line `▲ Detached from Codex · press Enter to reattach`. No hardware cursor: no program takes the keys. |
| `jackin_pane_exited` | Tab `6 exited ✓`. The Codex stream is not dimmed, the note row reads ` Codex exited (code 0) · the sandbox keeps running`, and the status bar reads `Codex · Primary · done`. |
| `jackin_pane_failed` | Tab `7 failed ●`. The empty state `Codex is not attached` (the link could not start, so there is no grid), the note row ` Could not attach to Codex: the gateway is unreachable · run jackin doctor · press Enter to retry`, the same text as the error status, and `Codex · Primary · needs input` in the status bar. |
| `jackin_close_pane_codex` | Tab `1 live`: Codex (left) beside a pager on the alternate screen. The dialog `Close pane?` says `Codex keeps running in the sandbox. This pane detaches.` with `▎Cancel` and `Close pane`. |
| `jackin_close_pane_shell` | The same tab with the pager pane focused: `Close pane?` says `The shell ends. Unsaved state in it is lost.` |
| `jackin_close_tab` | `Close tab?` says `Closes every pane in this tab. Codex panes keep running; shells end.` with `▎Cancel` and `Close tab`. |
| `jackin_paste_confirm` | A host paste of three lines into the Codex pane: `Paste 3 lines?` says `This pane has no paste protection, so each line runs as you paste it.` The buttons are `▎Cancel` and `Paste`: the dialog opens on Cancel, so a stray Enter pastes nothing. |
| `jackin_pane_info_title` | `Ctrl+B i` on the Codex pane: `Debug info` with `Sandbox`, `Role`, `Agent`, `Title  payments-platform · gpt-5-codex` (set by the program with OSC 2), `Workdir`, `Instance` and `jackin`. |

### Decisions and deviations

1. `PaneLink::drain(now_ms, output_budget)` takes the console's clock. The design has `drain(budget)`. The scripted `SimLink` and the replay window both play on the simulated clock, and a link with no clock argument would need a hidden second one. J5's `JackinPaneLink` ignores `now_ms`.
2. The link is opened by the pane, not by `Backend::spawn_pane`. `spawn_pane` still builds the body (B8a's `PaneSpawn` is unchanged); a `TerminalPane` holds a `LinkSource` and calls `open(request, size)` at the first size that fits (20 columns by 4 rows, the track column not counted), so no guessed size ever reaches the program. A refused open is `LinkStatus::Failed(reason)`, which is the `spawn_pane` failure of the brief; the lazy wait is `Pending`.
3. The console's words reach a pane when it is asked, not when it is built: `note`, `suffix`, `face` and `take_effects` take `&PaneWords`. This keeps `PaneSpawn` as it is and the words in `Vocabulary` (`Vocabulary.pane`, with the demo and the OpenShell values; the 21 words that equal in both sets are listed by prefix in `tests/vocabulary.rs`). The policy reaches the panes through `Daemon::set_policy`, called by `App::new` for every daemon, by `Go::Attach` and by `open_first_session`.
4. `PaneBody` gained `suffix`, `face`, `gesture`, `wheel`, `paste_confirmed`, `take_effects`, `set_policy` and `accepts_input`, each with a default that leaves a `Script` body as it was, and `PasteOutcome::Dropped`. `ViewportOps` gained `draws_track` so the Capsule does not draw a second bar over the track column of a terminal viewport. `Backend` gained `spawnable` and `pane_timing`.
5. The replay window (6.8 left it open): it opens when the `Attached` event is drained and closes at the first of 800 ms, or 600 ms with the output quiet for 150 ms, where quiet is counted from the last output or from the attach when none came. With no output it closes at 600 ms; with output at 550 ms, at 700 ms; with output every 100 ms, at the 800 ms cap. The tests pin each of the four (N22).
6. An OSC 52 payload that is not valid base64, or whose bytes are not UTF-8, or is empty, is dropped without a status (N20). A refusal, whatever its cause, shows its status at most once every 10 s per pane. `Copied from Codex · {n} characters` is `Request::Copy` with the `what` text, so the native sink decides between `Copied` and `Copy requested · this terminal may ignore OSC 52` (B2c).
7. The gesture is the last key the pane forwarded (a key the filter drops is not one), its last paste, or a click or press in it. The wheel, a chord the Capsule keeps, and a key into a pane with no live program are not gestures. A gesture at or within 5,000 ms counts.
8. The confirm dialog of a multi-line paste opens on Cancel (`Dialog::initial_focus`), a deviation from `Dialog::confirm`'s default: a stray Enter must not run lines. Its title is `Paste {n} lines?` with `plural`, so one line reads `Paste 1 line?`. A paste that ends in a newline counts the lines of the cleaned text (`a\nb\n` is 2).
9. `Ctrl+B d` still only leaves the Capsule; no key sends `PaneCommand::Detach`. A shell pane is closed by a detach (design 6.4), so sending it for every pane on leaving would end the shells; the choice belongs to J5. `Go::Detach` does tell the panes they lost the focus (`Daemon::blur`).
10. The Capsule posts `Scrollback cleared` for `Clear pane` only from the Edit menu, as the demo does; the chord and the palette stay silent. `Redraw` keeps the `redrawn` flash for every pane and also asks a terminal pane to repaint (`PaneCommand::Redraw`).
11. A pane whose instance is missing from the roster still draws (the identity row and the status bar are empty, `capsule_panes::a_pane_outlives_its_instance_record`), but `World::tick` feeds only the daemons of running instances, so it no longer receives output. The design asks only for the draw.
12. `Go::Attach` builds the daemon from `Backend::initial_panes` whenever the instance has none, not only from the cockpit. The demo's daemons exist before the attach, so no approved baseline moved.
13. No `Msg::PaneWake`: the wake of a pane task is J5's. The Capsule pumps every pane on its tick.
14. `jackin-console` now enables the `terminal` feature of `junie-tui` (termpane), which it needs for `TerminalPane`; the manifest test of the product shape still holds.
15. The scenario's links play on the console's clock, and the Capsule syncs sizes only after it has drawn once (it learns the body then), so a paused journey draws the first frame (`Waiting for room`), then advances the clock. A live preview needs no such step.
16. Not done here, by design: `Msg::PaneWake`, the native clipboard sink, the transport's repaint nudge on each attach (D28's "each attach runs the redraw resize" is the transport's: `PaneCommand::Redraw` exists for `Ctrl+B r` and the Capsule never computes the nudge), and every J-side row of T43, T45 to T49 and T52.

```text
cargo clippy --locked --workspace --all-targets --all-features -- -D warnings               exit 0
cargo fmt --all --check                                                                     exit 0
git diff --name-status 29b9a6c -- tests/functional/baselines apps/jackin/tests/functional/baselines   11 lines, every one `A` (the 11 baselines above)
```

The skipped test is the quarantined `tablepro` write gate. Every test that exists on 29b9a6c, among them every approved baseline of the root crate and of `jackin-console`, passes unchanged. The red worktrees (29b9a6c and `f38e3cc`) were removed after the runs.

### Named breaks (32, every one red)

Each break is one exact text replacement applied and reverted by a script, which runs the tests of the file named and then `touch`es the restored file. The first run of this list forgot the `touch`: a restored file kept its old modification time, cargo took the broken build for fresh, and a later gate run failed two tests on a stale binary. The list was rerun after the fix and gave the same reds; the gate above is a fresh build.

## Batch fix round (BG, BG2, B8b)

Base `2206bba` (worktree `wt/fix4`). Inputs: the code review `P9.3-BG-BG2-B8b-review.md` (findings F1 to F4, F6, F8) and the UX review `P9.3-B8b-ux.md` (M1, M2). D27 holds: only `jackin_pane_exited` and `jackin_pane_failed` moved. Every test was written first and run on the unmodified source; each red below is the disagreement, not a compile error. Not in this round: F5, F7, F9, F10, UX M3 and the LOW items of the UX review (they stay open as the reviews list them).

### Fixes and red lines

| # | Finding | Fix | Test |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | F1 (MEDIUM 1) an OSC 52 write in the replay is copied, and the gesture survives a reattach | `TerminalPane::replaying()` (replay window open, or status `Attaching`) gates `ClipboardWrite`; `open_link` clears `last_gesture_ms` | `clipboard_policy`: `a_clipboard_write_in_the_replay_is_not_copied_after_a_key` (probe P9), `the_replay_of_a_reattach_is_not_copied` (probe P1), `a_reattach_forgets_the_key_before_it` (a write after the window, 1.5 s after a key on the old link, is refused with the blocked status; a key on the new link allows the next one) |
| 2 | F2 (MEDIUM 2) a replayed bell marks a background pane `Blocked` | `Bell` and `Notification` use the same `replaying()` gate | `attention`: `a_bell_in_the_replay_marks_nothing` (BEL and OSC 9, inside the window and after it) |
| 3 | F3 (MEDIUM 3) a paste into a full queue returns `Sent` | `send_input` returns `Sent`; `paste_with` maps `Queued` to `PasteOutcome::Sent`, `Full` to the new `PasteOutcome::Full`, `Closed` to `Dropped`; the capsule shows `pane.paste_full` as an error | `terminal_pane`: `a_paste_the_link_did_not_take_is_not_reported_sent`; `capsule_input`: `a_paste_the_link_did_not_take_says_so` |
| 4 | F4 (MEDIUM 4) a lone `\r` in a paste is deleted | `ForwardPolicy::clean` maps `\r\n` and `\r` to `\n` before it drops controls, so the line count and the confirm are right | `tests/forward_policy.rs`: `every_line_break_is_one_newline` |
| 5 | UX M1 the exited pane is a dead end | Note: `{agent} exited (code {code}) · press Enter to restart · the sandbox keeps running` (the reviewer's wording). Enter on an exited pane calls the new `LinkSource::restart`, which opens a link to a new run, as `jackin attach --restart` does (D09). `SimSource::restart` attaches and stays | `pane_states`: `enter_restarts_an_exited_program`, `exited_note_and_close`; journey `jackin_terminal_detached_exited_and_failed_panes` presses Enter on the exited tab after the failed baseline |
| 6 | UX M2 the failed note loses its key on a narrow pane | `failed`: `Press Enter to retry · could not attach to {agent}: {reason}`; `connection_lost`: `Gateway connection lost · press Enter to reattach` (49 characters, the reviewer's wording) | `pane_states`: `failed_note_and_retry`, `a_stream_that_closes_before_the_attach_is_a_failure`, `detached_note_and_enter` |
| 7 | F6 (LOW) a reattach below the floor opens at the stale size | `relink`: a pane under 20x4 goes `Pending` ("Pane too small") and opens at the first size that fits, with the same kind of attempt | `terminal_pane`: `a_reattach_below_the_floor_waits_for_room` |
| 8 | F8 (LOW) screen literals | `PaneWords` gains `paste_full`, `pasted` (four sites) and `send_prefix` | `capsule_input`: the existing `Pasted into the focused pane` and `Send Ctrl+B` assertions |

Design notes:

- Restart goes through `LinkSource`, not `PaneCommand`. An exited pane has dropped its link (`Exited` sets `link = None`), so there is no link to send a command into, and a restart is a new attach. `LinkSource::restart` is a required method (two implementors: `SimSource`, the test `Bench`). The brief said "through the PaneLink"; the reviewer allowed "a `PaneCommand` or `LinkSource` request". `SimLink` stays as it is and the sim source honours the request.
- Restart applies to any exited pane, a shell too. The exit note names the key for every pane, so none is a dead end. A shell restart opens a new shell; D09 only decides the Codex case.
- The demo vocabulary keeps its own `exited` and `connection_lost` (container and daemon words); the `pane.paste_full`, `pane.pasted` and `pane.send_prefix` words are the same in both.

Red (all on `2206bba` plus the test files only; `cargo nextest run --locked -j 4 --no-fail-fast -p jackin-console --all-features --test clipboard_policy --test attention --test terminal_pane --test pane_states --test capsule_input`, and `-p junie-tui --all-features --test forward_policy`; both exit 100):

```text
the_replay_of_a_reattach_is_not_copied                      left: [Copy { text: "Hello", what: "from Codex · 5 characters" }]   right: []
a_reattach_forgets_the_key_before_it                        left: [Copy { text: "Hello", what: "from Codex · 5 characters" }]   right: []

a_paste_the_link_did_not_take_is_not_reported_sent          assert_ne: left: Sent   right: Sent   (strengthened to PasteOutcome::Full with the fix)
```

## BG3: the launch gaps the jackin launch step found (J4)

Plan: the J4 notes of the jackin repository (`rewrite/reconciliation/P9.3.md`, section "J4", "Baseline gaps" 1 to 8) and the brief of BG3. Base `9ac0e55`. The rule behind every row is the one of BG and BG2: jackin writes no screen text, so every sentence comes from `LaunchWords` (the launch vocabulary a backend names in `Backend::launch_content()`, the `words` of `LaunchContent`) and every live fact arrives through a typed value. D27 holds: no approved baseline moved and the demo is byte-identical (`git status --porcelain` over both baseline directories is empty after the full run).

Tests: `apps/jackin/tests/gaps_bg3.rs` (feature `demo`, 14 tests: 10 for the gaps and 4 guards that pass before and after). The guards pin what must not move: `no_demo_world_gains_an_account_from_the_only_rule` (all 16 worlds, all 6 agents), `the_demo_leaves_through_the_outro_when_nothing_is_running`, `a_launch_without_a_warning_shows_no_docker_banner` and `the_banner_comes_from_the_setting_when_the_backend_sends_no_warning`. A test backend (`Scripted`) gives the cockpit a feed the test writes and the `LaunchContent` it names, so a test can show that a sentence comes from the words and not from the cockpit. Three older test files changed with the types: `launch_feed.rs` (`refused()` is a typed reason, the test is `a_refusal_from_the_feed_is_drawn_in_the_launch_words`, `LaunchFailure.next_step` is a `NextStep`), `launch_checks.rs` (the guarded simulator's refusal is a typed reason) and `library_has_no_demo.rs` (the three refusal sentences are now allowed in `domain/launch.rs`, where `LaunchWords` holds them, and still forbidden in `screens/cockpit.rs` and `launch.rs`; `the_cockpit_draws_no_refusal_of_its_own` checks both sides).

Facts checked before building:

- Gap 4 and the demo. A probe over the 16 worlds and the 6 agents looked for a world where the new rule would fire (no default, no discovered account, exactly one ready account). It found one: `openshell-fixed` for Amp. Amp is not launchable there (`Surfaces.agent_blocked`), and the offer of a blocked agent carries `configured`, which the new account would have flipped. The rule therefore runs after the two early returns of `offer_for` (`agent_blocked`, runtime mode `Ignore`), so the offers of blocked agents are byte-equal, and the committed guard finds no hit in any world.
- Gap 5 and the demo. Of the 16 scenarios, `first-use` is the only world with no running instance (`outro-last` starts in the outro by design): the failure acknowledgement ends in the outro there (probe on `Go::LaunchFailedAck`). No approved baseline reaches that path, but the demo does, so the demo keeps it through data: `LaunchContent.when_empty` is `EmptyLanding::Outro` in the simulator's demo content and `EmptyLanding::Manager` in `LaunchContent::openshell()`.
- Gap 3 and the baselines. A first version made every `PropsList` honour `Prop::wrap`. It moved three approved root baselines (`showcase_page_diskcleanup`, `showcase_page_textutilities`, `showcase_page_workspaces`: readouts such as `hardlinks counted once` that the approved look shows on one row; 5 cells in `diskcleanup`). The clip stays there: only the list inside an `InfoDialog` wraps (`PropsList::wrapping`, `pub(crate)`, set by `InfoDialog::new`), and an `InfoDialog` intro line wraps only when it does not fit its width. With that, all 215 root journeys and all 50 jackin journeys pass on their unchanged baselines. Every current failure and refusal fits one line, so `jackin_launch_failure` and `jackin_role_not_allowed` did not move.

Decisions the brief left open:

1. "Vocabulary" for the launch is `LaunchWords`. The cockpit reads `content.words`, and `LaunchContent::openshell()` is what jackin already uses, so the new sentences are fields of `LaunchWords` and not of `Vocabulary`.
2. `Refusal` is gone. `LaunchEvent::Refused(RefusalReason)` carries facts (`RoleNotAllowed { workspace, role }`, `AgentNotLaunchable { agent, launchable }`), and `LaunchWords::refusal(&reason) -> RefusalText { title, found, state, next_step }` words all four. jackin writes none of them, including `found`, which the J4 notes had as the planner's own text.
3. The demo's `NextStep::RunDoctor` is worded with the Docker bridge sentence the demo always had, and `LaunchContent::openshell()` uses `RunDoctor` for its network failure, so both approved texts are unchanged.
4. The guarded simulator does not refuse a non-Codex agent. `launch-checks` sets `agent_blocked`, and many of its tests launch Claude Code targets to test the role and env checks. The refusal of gap 6 is driven by a feed, which is what jackin sends.
5. The record of a cancelled launch that arrives late is `Msg::LaunchCancelled { instance: Box<Instance> }`, the baseline's own kind of reply (a backend raises it from `Backend::due` or the external channel). `World::absorb` replaces the row of the same id or adds it, so the synchronous record of `cancel` and the late one do not double.
6. `DockerCapability` is `{ risks: Vec<DockerRiskText> }`: the banner counts the risks and the `i` dialog lists them, so the event must carry them. The banner text is unchanged.

### Gap to API table, for the jackin side

| # | Gap | Baseline API | What jackin does now |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | `Refusal` has no typed words | `domain::launch::RefusalReason::{RoleNotAllowed { workspace, role }, AgentNotLaunchable { agent, launchable }}`. `LaunchEvent::Refused(RefusalReason)` replaces `Refused(Box<Refusal>)`; the struct `Refusal` is removed. `LaunchWords::refusal(&RefusalReason) -> RefusalText`, from the fields `refused_state` and `role_not_allowed`, `agent_not_launchable` (`RefusalWords { title, found, next_step }`, templates take `{workspace}`, `{role}`, `{agent}`, `{launchable}`). `LaunchContent::openshell()` carries the approved texts; the demo's `words()` carries the same for the two sentences the demo shows. | Sends `Refused(RefusalReason::RoleNotAllowed { workspace: name, role })` and writes no title, `found`, `state` or next step. |
| 2 | `LaunchFailure.next_step` is a `String` | `LaunchFailure.next_step: NextStep`, `NextStep::{None, Retry { sandbox, launch }, RunDoctor, FixCredential}`. `LaunchWords::next_step(&NextStep) -> Option<String>`, from `next_retry` (`sandbox {sandbox} remains; retry with: jackin load --launch-id {launch}`), `next_doctor` and `next_credential`. `NextStep::None` draws no `Next step` row. `LaunchContent::openshell().network_failure.next_step` is `RunDoctor`. | Maps `LaunchNextStep::Retry { sandbox, launch }` to `NextStep::Retry { sandbox: name, launch: id }`, a failure with no remaining sandbox to `None`, a credential failure to `FixCredential`, a failed gateway to `RunDoctor`. `LaunchNextStep`'s `Display` is no longer needed by the console. |
| 3 | The failure dialog clips `summary` and `Next step` | No new API. `InfoDialog` wraps an intro line that does not fit, and its props list honours `Prop::wrap`. | Nothing. |
| 4 | `offer_for(None)` offers nothing with one account and no default | `AccountRegistry::only_ready(provider)` and the last rule of `World::offer_for` with no workspace: no default, no discovered account, exactly one ready account of the provider. The account is offered and preselected. A default that cannot be used is not replaced. | Nothing; a launch from a directory with one ready Codex account and no default starts with it. |
| 5 | A failed or cancelled launch with nothing running ends in the outro | `LaunchContent.when_empty: EmptyLanding::{Outro, Manager}`. `Go::LaunchFailedAck` with no running instance goes to the manager when the backend's content says `Manager`, selecting the record of the launch when there is one. `LaunchContent::openshell()` says `Manager`; the simulator's demo content says `Outro`. | Uses `LaunchContent::openshell()`; builds its own content only by `..LaunchContent::openshell()`. Compile break for a literal `LaunchContent { .. }`. |
| 6 | No typed refusal for a non-Codex agent | `RefusalReason::AgentNotLaunchable { agent, launchable }` and its words (`agent_not_launchable`: `Agent not launchable`, `{agent} cannot be launched: {launchable} is the only agent jackin launches.`, `Launch with {launchable}; {agent} stays usage only.`). The cockpit draws it like any refusal: no stage, no record, Esc goes to the manager. | Sends `Refused(AgentNotLaunchable { agent: target.agent, launchable: Agent::Codex })` before any stage when `target.agent` is not Codex (D04). |
| 7 | `LaunchFeed::cancel` cannot return a record that needs the sandbox | `Msg::LaunchCancelled { instance: Box<Instance> }`. `World::absorb` replaces the row with the same id or adds it; the manager rebuilds its rows. `cancel` may return `None` and the record follows as this reply. | `cancel` returns `None`; the launch task sends `Msg::LaunchCancelled` with the record when the sandbox is known. |
| 8 | No `Warning` event | `LaunchEvent::Warning(DockerCapability)`, `domain::docker::DockerCapability { risks: Vec<DockerRiskText> }`. The cockpit draws the banner and lists the risks from the event when the backend sent one, else from `Surfaces.docker` as before. | Sends `Warning(DockerCapability { risks })` for the session's `LaunchWarning::DockerCapability`, with the risk texts it already puts in `Surfaces.docker`. |

Compile breaks for jackin: `LaunchEvent::Refused` (payload type), `Refusal` (removed), `LaunchFailure.next_step`, the new `LaunchEvent::Warning` and `Msg::LaunchCancelled` variants in any exhaustive match, and `LaunchWords` and `LaunchContent` literals (7 new fields on `LaunchWords`, one on `LaunchContent`).

### Red

The red tree is the finished code with one text edit to production code per break (`unwire.py`, in the task scratchpad, not committed): the file is edited, `cargo nextest run --locked -j 4 -p jackin-console --all-features --no-fail-fast --test gaps_bg3 --test launch_feed --test launch_checks --test library_has_no_demo` is run, the file is restored and its sha256 is compared with the one before. The guards pass in every run. `LaunchFailure.next_step` as a `String` and `Refusal` with its four strings are the old types: the tests do not compile against them, and a compile error is not a disagreement, so each red below is a behaviour the types alone do not give.

| Break | Result | Failing tests | First |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `REFUSAL-words-empty`: `refusal()` returns an empty title and next step (the J4 observation) | red, `69 tests run: 62 passed, 7 failed` | `a_refusal_is_worded_by_the_launch_words`, and `a_refusal_from_the_feed_is_drawn_in_the_launch_words`, `the_sim_refuses_a_role_the_workspace_does_not_allow`, `the_checks_sit_over_the_codex_cockpit_of_seven_stages`, `an_agent_jackin_does_not_launch_is_refused_in_the_launch_words`, `a_launch_that_leaves_nothing_returns_to_the_manager`, `the_demo_leaves_through_the_outro_when_nothing_is_running` | `"Role not allowed" missing:` (the dialog shows `State  nothing was created` and an empty `Next step` row); the others `never reached: the refusal` |

## BG4: the account-source gaps the jackin account step found (J3)

Plan: the J3 notes of the jackin repository (`rewrite/reconciliation/P9.3.md`, section "J3", "Baseline gaps" 1 to 7) and the brief of BG4. Base `6870a19`. The rule behind every row is the one of BG, BG2 and BG3: jackin writes no screen text, so every sentence comes from `Vocabulary` and every fact arrives through a typed value. D27 holds: no approved baseline moved and the demo is byte-identical (`git status --porcelain` over both baseline directories is empty after the full run).

Tests: `apps/jackin/tests/gaps_bg4.rs` (feature `demo`, 15 tests). Six older test files changed with the types, none in what it asserts: `pending.rs` (the canary of the check reply is a `Found::EnvSet`, the rejected check is `IssueReason::KeyEmpty`, `ChangeSet` has the two new fields), `effects.rs` (the refresh canary is `RefreshOutcome::NeedsAttention`, the broker issue is compared as `IssueReason::BrokerDown`), `fixed_fields.rs`, `account_data.rs` (`Usability::label` takes the `Vocabulary`), `product_support` (`accounts()` returns a `Vec`) and `vocabulary.rs` (below).

Facts checked before building:

- Every text the demo and the simulator wrote for an issue, a check row or a refresh outcome was listed first (`sim/provider.rs`, `fixtures/mod.rs`). Each is now a value plus a template. `the_demo_keeps_every_text_the_simulator_wrote` and `the_demo_keeps_every_key_text_the_simulator_wrote` word seven folder outcomes and six key outcomes of `provider::validate` and compare issue and rows with the old literal, and `the_demo_worlds_keep_every_issue_text` compares the issues and invalid validations of all 17 fixture worlds with the nine old sentences. The literals in the tests were copied from the code of `6870a19`. They pass on the finished code.
- A fact that only the demo knows (`mode 000 · owner root`, `Timed out after 8 s`, `~/.kimi has no api key`) is a field of the value (`detail`, `path` and `file`), the way `Notice::GatewayUnreachable.reason` and `DoctorFailure::Failed` already carry what the host reported. A template that shows it is `{detail}`.
- `OpError` already names the 1Password cases, so `IssueReason::OnePassword(OpError)` carries them and `Vocabulary::op_error` words them. `OpError::message()` stays for the one place that holds no `Vocabulary` (the picker of a reference) and returns `ReasonWords::openshell().op_error(self)`, so the words exist once.
- The demo and the OpenShell set share the words of `ReasonWords` (as they share `PaneWords`), so `the_openshell_vocabulary_renames_the_demo_words` skips the `reason.` fields like the `pane.` fields.
- `InspectChanges` is not in the showcase catalog (`src/catalog.rs` lists no `inspect_changes` item), so the new fields add no coverage line.

Decisions the brief left open:

1. The reason enum is `IssueReason`, not `Reason`: `junie_tui::core::refusal::Reason` is the fixed-control reason and both are imported in `account.rs`.
2. One value, two wordings. A reason is worded as an issue line (`Vocabulary::reason`) and as a check row (`Vocabulary::check`); the simulator already said the same fault differently in the two places (`API key required: the field is empty` and `Field is empty`). `ReasonWords` holds a `Phrase { issue, check }` for each.
3. A passed row is a `Found` (not an `IssueReason`): `ReferenceResolved`, `KeyPresent`, `Detected(DetectedKind)`, `Unparsable`, `Authenticated`, `WindowsReadable`, `EnvSet`, `FileFound`, `FolderFound`, `Login` and `Level(ValidationLevel)`.
4. The D25 reasons are `UsageReason::{NoCredentials, ExpiredLogin, Unsupported, RateLimited { retry_in_secs }, Network { detail }, Provider { detail }}` under `IssueReason::Usage`. The network error of the brief is `UsageReason::Network`. The detail of `Unsupported` is dropped.
5. `Msg::AccountRefreshed` and `AccountValidated` keep their `Err(String)` (the error text of a failed reply, shown by `cx.error`). The issue that `World::absorb` makes from it is `Usage(Provider { detail })`.
6. `Usability` gains `Failed(IssueReason)` for a `Lifecycle::Error` account with an issue, so `Usability::label` and `ValidationState::label` take the `Vocabulary`.
7. `AccountForm::default()` is the demo's four providers and `AccountForm::openshell()` lists `Provider::ALL` (new, 14 variants; the test `the_account_form_lists_every_provider` matches every variant without a wildcard, so a new `Provider` breaks it). A label is `{agent} · {provider}` when the provider has an agent and the provider label otherwise, the same rule the four demo labels follow. The select popup scrolls (it shows eight rows).
8. `ChangeSet.unread` is a list of `UnreadFile { path, status }`. The widget draws an unread file as a row (compact) and a tree leaf (advanced) with the word where the counts go; Enter on it opens nothing. `omitted` follows the summary line. The words are `ChangeWords { omitted, unread }` (library default `{count} more not listed` and `not read`), set by `InspectChanges::words`; the capsule fills them from `Vocabulary.changes_omitted` and `changes_unread`.
9. `Effect::RefreshAccount.stagger_ms` stays. The console spaces the requests of a refresh of several accounts (160 ms on the Accounts page, 120 ms on the Usage page) because the simulator staggers its replies; a backend that serializes its refreshes (jackin does: one report at a time) ignores the field. `refreshing_every_account_staggers_the_requests` pins that the effect still carries it.

### Gap to API table, for the jackin side

| # | Gap | Baseline API | What jackin does now |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | `RecoverableIssue.message`, `CheckRow` text and `Refresh.outcome` are free strings | `domain::reason::{IssueReason, UsageReason, Found, RefreshOutcome}`. `RecoverableIssue::new(code, IssueReason, recoverability)` and the field `reason` replace `message`. `CheckRow::{Ok(Found), Failed(IssueReason), Skipped(IssueReason)}`. `Refresh.outcome: RefreshOutcome`. `Vocabulary.reason: ReasonWords` with `Vocabulary::{reason, check, found, refresh_outcome, op_error}`. The value names the case and its facts; the template takes them. | `SourceFault`: `EnvName` to `EnvInvalid { name }`, `EnvUnset` to `EnvMissing { name }`, `EnvEmpty` to `EnvEmpty { name }`, `FileMissing` to `FileMissing { path }`, `FileNotRegular` to `FileNotRegular { path }`, `Unreadable`, `Login(..)` and `Fs(..)` to `FolderUnreadable { path, detail }` (the host's text), `FolderMissing` to `FolderMissing { path }`, `NoLogin` to `LoginMissing { path, file: "auth.json" }`, `LoginInvalid` and `NotChatgpt` to `LoginInvalid { file: "auth.json" }`, `Reference` to `BadReference { reference }`, `Op(fault)` to `OnePassword(op_error(fault))`, `NotConcealed` to `OnePassword(WrongFieldShape)`, `EmptyField` to `OnePassword(EmptyMaterial)`. `SourceFound`: `Env` to `Found::EnvSet`, `File` to `FileFound`, `Folder` to `FolderFound`, `Login { account }` to `Found::Login { account }`, `OnePassword` to `ReferenceResolved`; a quota read to `Found::Level(QuotaReadable)`. A skipped row is `Skipped(IssueReason::Skipped)`. `UnavailableReason` to `Usage(..)`: `NoCredentials`, `ExpiredLogin`, `Unsupported`, `RateLimited { retry_in_secs: retry_at - now }`, `Network { detail }`, `Provider { detail }`. `Freshness` to `RefreshOutcome::{Current, Stale, Failed}`. No `OpError::message()`, `Display` or `error_chain` text reaches a row. Still strings, not part of this gap: `RecoverableIssue.detail`, `QuotaWindow.note` (jackin may word a sentinel note with `Vocabulary::reason`), and the `Err(String)` of a reply. |
| 2 | `AccountForm.providers` is four | `Provider::ALL`; `AccountForm::openshell()` lists all 14 with `{agent} · {provider}` labels (the provider label when it has no agent). `AccountForm::default()` stays the demo's four. | Takes the `openshell()` form from `Surfaces::openshell`; Cursor, Gemini, OpenRouter, Z.AI, MiniMax, Amp, Kimi, Antigravity, Hermes and Muse accounts can be added and edited. |
| 3 | `OpItemRow.fields` and `OpVaultRow.access` have no source in `op` | `OpItemRow.fields: Option<usize>`, `OpVaultRow.access: Option<VaultAccess>`. `None` draws no `{n} fields` (the row reads `{category}`) and no access tag. | Sends `fields: None`; sends `access: Some(Denied)` when `op item list` is refused and `None` otherwise (read-only cannot be seen). |
| 4 | `OnePassword::accounts()` is a borrowed slice | `fn accounts(&self) -> Vec<OpAccount>`. `OpFlow::new` starts at the vaults when it lists exactly one. | Returns the accounts of the shared `OpSnapshot` (`snapshot.lock().accounts.clone()`), so the picker starts at the vaults of a single account. |
| 5 | `ChangeSet` has no `omitted` and no unread flag | `ChangeSet.omitted: usize`, `ChangeSet.unread: Vec<UnreadFile>`, `UnreadFile { path, status }`, `ChangeWords { omitted, unread }`, `InspectChanges::words`, `Vocabulary.changes_omitted` (takes `{count}`) and `changes_unread`. `ChangeSet::is_empty` is false for a set with only an omitted count or an unread file. The demo sets 0 and an empty list. | Maps `Session::changes` (at most 500 untracked files, bounded reads) to `omitted` and `unread`. Compile break: any `ChangeSet { .. }` literal. |
| 6 | `Effect::RefreshAccount.stagger_ms` asks for a delay | No change. The field stays; see decision 9. | Ignores it (one report at a time behind a mutex). |

Compile breaks for jackin: `RecoverableIssue::new` and `.message`, `CheckRow` and `Refresh.outcome` (payload types), `ValidationState::label(&Vocabulary)`, `Usability::label(fixed, &Vocabulary)` and the new `Usability::Failed` variant, `OnePassword::accounts` (return type), `OpVaultRow.access` and `OpItemRow.fields`, `ChangeSet` literals, and `Vocabulary` literals (`reason`, `changes_omitted`, `changes_unread`).

## BG5: the Capsule pane gaps the jackin pane step found (J5)

Plan: the J5 notes of the jackin repository (`rewrite/reconciliation/P9.3.md`, section "J5: baseline gaps", items 1 to 4 and the `refresh_keeps_open_panes` row of item 6) and the brief of BG5. Base `07f76db`. The rule behind every row is the one of BG to BG4: jackin writes no screen text, so every sentence comes from `PaneWords` and every live fact arrives through a typed value. D27 holds: no approved baseline moved and the demo is byte-identical (`git status --porcelain` over both baseline directories is empty after the full run). The failed note keeps the key-first order of the batch fix round (`Press Enter to retry · could not attach to Codex: the gateway is unreachable · run jackin doctor`), so `jackin_pane_failed` (rows 37 and 39) was not re-taken and no approved text changed.

Tests: `apps/jackin/tests/gaps_bg5.rs` (feature `demo`, 10 tests, on the Capsule rig of `tests/capsule_support` and the test links of `tests/pane_support`), and two tests added to `tests/capsule_spawn.rs` for gap 2 (it already holds the picker row journey). Older tests changed with the types, none in what it asserts: `pane_states.rs`, `terminal_pane.rs`, `attention.rs`, `capsule_panes.rs` (a failure is a `LinkFault`), `capsule_spawn.rs` (the refusal is a `PaneRefusal`), and the two support modules (`Bench::refuse_with(LinkFault)`, `TermBackend::left()`; the unused `TermBackend.refuse` field is gone).

Facts checked before building:

- The runtime (`junie_tui::runtime`) already drains the whole external queue before it draws, so a redraw is one `dirty` flag per loop pass. A wake therefore needs only to answer `Changed` once: the first wake drains the pane and the others find nothing and answer `Ignored`. `wake_is_coalesced` pins that, and that output which arrives after the redraw asks for the next one.
- The Capsule leaves through `App::go` for every page (Ctrl+B d is `Go::Detach`, the quit dialog and the end of an instance are `Go::InstanceEnded`, the host menu is `Go::Manager`). `App::go` reads the open Capsule before it navigates and calls `Backend::capsule_left` once if no Capsule of that instance is open afterwards. A picker, a dialog, a zoom, a tab switch or a tick never changes the route, so none calls it. A wake does not either.
- The picker row clipped the first wording tried, `Codex is already open in this san…` (the detail column is about 33 columns), so the OpenShell reason is `One {agent} pane per sandbox` (26 columns for Codex), not a sentence.
- `Snapshot.instances[].daemon` is the one daemon field a snapshot carries. `World::apply` rebuilds it from `World.daemons` (`refresh_snapshots`), so a snapshot that says `Unavailable` for every record leaves the tabs and the roster rows intact. `refresh_keeps_open_panes` sends exactly that snapshot, as a `Msg::Refreshed` and as a `Remote::Reloaded`.

Decisions the brief left open:

1. The reason enum is `PaneRefusal::MainProcessOpen { agent }` (one pane for each main process, D28). It is the only variant; a second reason is a new variant and a new word. `Backend::spawnable` returns `Result<(), PaneRefusal>`; the `Reason` type of `junie_tui::core::refusal` (a free string) is no longer used by the backend.
2. `Backend::capsule_left(&self, world: &World, instance: &str)` is a method with a no-op default, not a `Msg`: the backend is the one that holds the links, and a message would have to travel through the world's queue before it could act.
3. A failure is a `LinkFault { reason: String, next: FailNext }` (`FailNext::{None, RunDoctor}`). `reason` stays the host's own text, like `Notice::GatewayUnreachable.reason`. `LinkFault::new(reason)` and `.then(next)` build one. `LinkStatus::Failed`, `EndKind::{Failed, Refused}` and the `Err` of `LinkSource::open` and `restart` carry it; there is no `From<String>`, so jackin's compile breaks name every site.
4. The next step is worded by `PaneWords::failure(agent, &fault)`: the existing `failed` template, then `failed_next` (`{failed} · {next}`) with `next_doctor` (`run jackin doctor`) when `next` is `RunDoctor`. The status line (`PaneEffect::Status`) and the note under the pane use the same function. An empty reason is still the `stream_closed` words, with no next step.
5. The demo's refusal in `capsule-terminal` is now `LinkFault::new("the gateway is unreachable").then(FailNext::RunDoctor)`: the same screen text, built from a value and the words.

### Gap to API table, for the jackin side

| # | Gap | Baseline API | What jackin does now |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | No wake for a pane task | `Msg::PaneWake { instance: InstanceId, pane: PaneId }`, sent as `Remote::Msg(..)` through the `ExternalSender` (or `App::deliver`). `World::wake_pane(&str, PaneId) -> bool` drains that pane and answers whether it had something new; `App` answers `Changed` only then. `PaneId` is `PaneSpawn.id`. | The pane task sends a wake when it puts output or an event in the inbox. It may send one for every chunk: a wake that finds nothing costs no redraw. Compile break: any exhaustive `match` on `Msg`. |
| 2 | `Backend::spawnable` has no typed reason | `Backend::spawnable(&World, &Instance, &PaneKind) -> Result<(), PaneRefusal>`, `PaneRefusal::MainProcessOpen { agent: Agent }`, `PaneWords.main_open` (`One {agent} pane per sandbox`, takes `{agent}`), `PaneWords::refusal(&PaneRefusal) -> String`. The picker row is disabled with that text as its detail. | Returns `Err(PaneRefusal::MainProcessOpen { agent: Agent::Codex })` for a second Codex pane of an instance and writes no text. Compile break: the return type of `spawnable`; `PaneWords` literals (three new fields). |
| 3 | No call when the Capsule is left | `Backend::capsule_left(&self, world: &World, instance: &str)`, default no-op. Called once when the Capsule of `instance` is no longer open after `App::go`: Ctrl+B d, any page, the end of the instance. | Detaches the Codex links of `instance` in `capsule_left` and stops reading `Daemon.attached_by` in `due`. Shells stay. Quitting the process drops the links (detach by drop), as before. |
| 4 | No typed next step for the failed-link state | `LinkFault { reason, next }`, `FailNext::{None, RunDoctor}`, `LinkFault::{new, then}`. `LinkSource::{open, restart} -> Result<Box<dyn PaneLink>, LinkFault>`, `EndKind::{Failed, Refused}(LinkFault)`, `LinkStatus::Failed(LinkFault)`. `PaneWords.failed_next` (`{failed} · {next}`), `PaneWords.next_doctor` (`run jackin doctor`), `PaneWords::failure`. | Wraps the text of `error_chain(error)` in `LinkFault::new(..)` and adds `.then(FailNext::RunDoctor)` for a gateway fault. Compile break: the three signatures and payloads above. |
| 5 | A refresh must keep open panes | No API: `Snapshot` has no daemon field, and `World::apply` rebuilds `Instance.daemon` from `World.daemons`. | Keeps sending a `Snapshot` with every `Instance.daemon` `Unavailable`; the console shows the open tabs. `refresh_keeps_open_panes` is the guard. |

## BG6: the account form offers an environment-variable source (P9.5 live test)

Plan: the finding of the P9.5 live test (the console's account form has no `env:` source, so a non-Codex account that reads a host variable, jackin DESIGN D26 `--credential env:NAME`, could only be added with the CLI) and the brief of BG6. Base `916de2c`. The rule behind every row is the one of BG to BG5: jackin writes no screen text, so every sentence comes from `Vocabulary` and every fact arrives through a typed value.

D27 and the demo: the demo form keeps its three sources (`the_demo_form_keeps_its_three_sources`, and no other functional baseline moved: the jackin functional run was 48 passed, 2 failed, and the 2 are the journeys that hold the four files). The new source exists only in `AccountForm::openshell()`. It follows `Plain-text API key`, so the keystrokes of the three journeys that walk the radio hold. Adding a row to that form moves exactly four approved openshell-fixed baselines, found by taking out the earlier `app.visual` calls of a scratch copy of the journeys one at a time (the harness stops at the first mismatch); the owner allowed the reviewed re-take of those four (below).

Tests: `apps/jackin/tests/gaps_bg6.rs` (feature `demo`, 14 tests), `apps/jackin/tests/gaps_bg6_words.rs` (2 tests), one PTY journey `jackin_fixed_account_form_offers_an_environment_variable` with the new baseline `jackin_fixed_account_form_env`. Two older tests changed with the data, neither in what it asserts: `surfaces.rs` (`surfaces_openshell_sets_every_field` lists the fourth label) and `vocabulary.rs` (`the_openshell_vocabulary_renames_the_demo_words` skips the `source.` words, as it skips `reason.`; the demo form never shows them).

Facts checked before building:

- Second item (instance card): `manager.rs` sent every `DaemonSnapshot` except `Tabs` to `daemon.card_unavailable`, so a running sandbox with no Capsule tab (`NoTabs`) read `running 2 h 14 min · gateway unreachable` next to a healthy gateway. `DaemonWords.card_no_tabs` is the new word and the card has an arm per case. OpenShell says `running {duration} · no Capsule tabs open` (the wording of `no_tabs` and `list_no_tabs`). The demo's `card_no_tabs` is its `card_unavailable` text, so a `NoTabs` card in the demo reads as it did. No demo fixture builds `NoTabs` (`grep NoTabs` finds only `panes.rs`, which reports it for a Capsule with no tabs, and tests), and no approved baseline row, in either baseline directory, holds `daemon unavailable`, `running … · unavailable` or `gateway unreachable`, so no approved file shows that card.
- `CredentialSource::HostEnv { var, detected }` already is the baseline's equivalent of jackin's `env:NAME`. `SourceKind` had no case for it, so `CredentialSource::kind()` returned `None` and the form could neither offer nor edit it. `SourceKind::HostEnv` closes that.
- `IssueReason::{EnvMissing, EnvEmpty, EnvInvalid}` and `Found::EnvSet` exist (BG4) and are reused. The simulator did not produce them: it checked a variable name as a folder (`Folder not found: ZAI_API_KEY`). `sim::provider::validate_env` now answers with them.
- `find_duplicate` had no case for a variable; `finish_save` passed a dummy `Folder` probe with an empty path. `DuplicateProbe::HostEnv { provider, var }` replaces it.
- Echo risk (owner instruction): a user can paste a key into the name field, and `EnvInvalid { name }` was worded `{name} is not a valid environment variable name`, which would print the key. The issue line is now `Not a valid variable name · letters, digits and _ only` and the check row `Not a valid variable name`; neither takes `{name}`. The variant still carries `name`, so the jackin side keeps compiling.

### Jackin-side API

| Need | Baseline API | What jackin does |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Offer the source | `AccountForm::openshell()` lists `SourceKind::HostEnv` after `Plain-text API key`, labelled `SourceWords::env_label` | `Surfaces::openshell` already takes the form from `AccountForm::openshell()`; nothing to change |
| Words | `Vocabulary.source: SourceWords { env_label, env_placeholder, env_help, env_empty }`, listed by `fields()` as `source.*`. The demo and OpenShell share them, as they share `reason.` | Struct literals of `Vocabulary` gain `source: SourceWords::openshell()` |
| Check | `Effect::CheckSource { source: CredentialSource::HostEnv { var, detected: DetectedKind::Unknown }, key: None, .. }`: the trimmed name, never a value | Validate `env:NAME`; reply `EnvInvalid { name }`, `EnvMissing { name }`, `EnvEmpty { name }` or `CheckRow::Ok(Found::EnvSet { name })` |
| Save | the account carries `CredentialSource::HostEnv { var, detected }`; no `ProbeFolder` effect | Store `env:NAME` |
| Duplicate | `DuplicateProbe::HostEnv { provider, var }` | none |

Compile breaks for jackin: `SourceKind` (new case), `DuplicateProbe` (new case) and `Vocabulary` literals (`source`).
